Our primary contribution is a methodology for analysis of semantic drift and discourse evolution in the embedding space itself. We also demonstrate modifications to existing methods that enable this analysis at scale, and we propose and demonstrate a null model comparison test to filter spurious dynamics. Key findings suggest that politically and socially contentious topics exhibit significant directional drift in embedding space, with inter-topic distances changing systematically over time beyond what the null model can explain, whereas domains such as music and sports remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [72] [ChildSafeAds Shared Task 2026: Commercial Content in Child-Facing YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19165)
*Thales Bertaglia,Catalina Goanta,Gerasimos Spanakis,Gunes Acar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ChildSafeAds是一个共享任务，旨在分析YouTube视频中可能触达儿童和青少年的商业内容，包含3,360个视频，利用SponsorBlock片段、转录文本、视频和频道信息及链接页面，要求系统识别推广优惠类型、产品类别和法律风险标记，并评估不同证据访问层级的成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是解决YouTube视频中针对儿童和青少年的商业内容缺乏有效监管和披露的问题，特别是45.5%的视频未正确使用平台广告披露标签，需要自动化系统来检测和分类此类内容，保护未成年人免受不当商业影响。

Method: 方法包括：收集SponsorBlock用户标记的赞助片段，配对转录文本、视频/频道元数据及描述中链接的销售页面；构建四个累积证据访问层级（从仅转录文本到完整链接页面）；定义三个子任务：ST1识别推广优惠类型，ST2分配产品类别，ST3识别法律风险标记；专家团队迭代设计分类法、提示和模型选择，使用GPT-5.4生成标签，GPT-5.6-luna独立标注开发集。

Result: 结果显示45.5%的视频未正确使用'包含付费推广'标签；构建了包含3,360视频的数据集，支持三个子任务评估；不同证据层级下的系统性能可与数据收集成本权衡；GPT模型辅助标注实现了高质量标签。

Conclusion: 结论是ChildSafeAds为检测面向儿童的YouTube商业内容提供了基准任务和数据集，揭示了广告披露合规性低的问题，多层次证据设计允许成本效益分析，未来将发布参赛系统结果以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: ChildSafeAds is a shared task on commercial content in YouTube videos likely to reach children and teenagers. It contains 3,360 videos from 939 channels. Each instance begins with a segment submitted to SponsorBlock, an open-source crowdsourced browser extension whose users mark sponsor segments so that others can skip them. We pair the segment with its available transcript, video and channel information, and a sales or service page linked from the video description. Systems determine what kind of offer is being promoted (ST1), assign product categories (ST2), and identify legal risk flags (ST3). The evidence is divided into four cumulative access levels, from the transcript to the linked page, so results can be compared against the cost of collecting the data. 45.5\% of videos in our data failed to properly use the in-platform ad disclosure method (the ``Includes paid promotion'' label). GPT-5.4 produced the labels after the expert organiser team reviewed samples and iterated on the taxonomy, prompts and model choices. GPT-5.6-luna independently labelled the development set. This report describes the task, data and evaluation. An updated version will add participating systems and shared-task results.

</details>


### [73] [SPADE: Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19197)
*Bo Liu,Simon Yu,Yiding Jiang,Ao Qu,Andrew Zhao,Zichen Liu,Junsu Kim,Zijian Zhou,Seungone Kim,Tongzheng Ren,Mickel Liu,Hanfei Yu,Zhaorun Chen,Weiyan Shi,Paul Pu Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPADE 是一个自我博弈强化学习框架，让单个大语言模型同时扮演环境设计师和推理智能体，通过可执行代码生成的动态环境实现开放式自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言智能体的训练环境池（人工策划、静态合成或固定验证器）导致目标分布固定，无法随学习者扩展而自适应增长，限制了持续自我改进的能力。

Method: SPADE 引入自我博弈机制：环境设计师编写具有 OpenAI Gym 风格 reset()/step() 接口的完整、长视界可执行环境代码（包含状态转移、奖励函数和验证代码）；推理智能体在其中学习行动。通过推理智能体在有/无特权提示下的奖励差距估计遗憾值，环境设计师优化该遗憾信号，生成处于智能体能力边缘且可行的环境。关键组件包括：用大规模预训练语料文档 grounding 环境设计师，并赋予其累积环境记忆。

Result: 在 30B 参数模型上，SPADE 在八个 held-out 数学、科学、代码和推理基准上平均比最强固定环境基线提升 +5.3；工具使用设置上 BFCL-v4 多轮提升 +5.7，ACEBench-Agent 提升 +13.9；游戏设置下优势随模型规模增长而扩大。

Conclusion: 通过将环境设计本身作为可学习组件，SPADE 为开放式自我改进迈出了关键一步，实现了目标分布的自适应扩展和持续能力提升。

Abstract: Continuous self-improvement requires an ever-expanding pool of self-generated, diverse, adaptive goals. For language agents, existing training environment pools (hand-curated, statically synthesized, or frozen-verifier) keep the goal distribution fixed as the learner scales. We introduce SPADE (Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments), a self-play RL framework in which a single LLM plays two roles: an Environment Designer that writes complete, long-horizon training environments as executable code with an OpenAI Gym-style reset()/step() interface, and a Reasoning Agent that learns to act in them. Each is a stateful, multi-turn environment (state transitions, reward functions, and verification code), so one interface spans reasoning problems and multi-step agentic tool use. The Reasoning Agent's regret is estimated using the gap between its reward with and without privileged hints; in optimizing this regret signal the Environment Designer learns to target environments at the edge of the agent's capabilities while keeping them feasible. Through extensive experimentation, we find several components critical to success: grounding the Environment Designer on documents sampled from a large pretraining corpus, and giving it an accumulated environment memory. Scaling to 30B-parameter models, SPADE improves over the strongest fixed-environment baseline by +5.3 on average across eight held-out math, science, code, and reasoning benchmarks, and lifts the tool-use setting by +5.7 on BFCL-v4 multi-turn and +13.9 on ACEBench-Agent; on the games setting, the margin over the strongest baseline grows with model scale. By making environment design itself a learnable component, SPADE takes a concrete step toward open-ended self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [Global Crises and National Policies: A Large Scale Analysis of Political Content in German Language Online Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18268)
*Yara Döring,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文利用自动化政治文本分析方法，研究2019-2022年德语在线媒体（报纸和推特）的政治内容，发现国际危机（COVID-19、俄乌战争）能同步化古典媒体的政治议程，暂时抹平国家和媒体形式差异；报纸内容更稳定，推特呈事件驱动的短时尖峰。自动化分析可提升透明度，帮助公民识别偏见，促进平衡公共讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法推荐导致政治偏见的媒体消费模式，自动化提取政治议程可揭示和分析在线媒体的政治偏见，从而促进政治中立的媒体消费。

Method: 采用现代政治文本分析方法，对2019-2022年德语在线媒体（数百万篇文章和推文）进行自动化细粒度政治偏见分析，涵盖COVID-19大流行和俄乌战争爆发等重大事件。

Result: 发现德瑞两国报道在国际事件驱动类别上主题相似，在国内政策影响类别上存在差异；报纸和推特在疫情期间围绕共享核心趋同，但强度和时间动态不同：报纸政治内容更稳定，推特呈短暂事件驱动尖峰；国际危机是古典媒体政治内容的强同步力量，暂时覆盖国家和媒体形式差异。

Conclusion: 自动化政治分析通过使在线媒体政治议程透明化，赋能公民；这种透明度也使媒体机构能弥合算法驱动的回声室与更知情、平衡的公共话语之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Today most media content is consumed based on algorithmic recommendations. Evidence suggests that this can lead to politically biased media consumption patterns. Automated extraction of political agendas from texts can reveal and analyze political biases in online media -- and thus help fostering politically unbiased media consumption. Here we employ modern political text analysis methods demonstrating the potential of automated fine-grained political bias analysis in online media. We conduct an analysis of political content in German language online media during the period 2019--2022, encompassing several million articles and tweets covering events with profound societal impact globally and nationally, the COVID-19 pandemic and the beginning of the war in Ukraine. Our analysis identifies thematic similarity between national (German and Swiss) reporting, particularly for categories driven by international events. We also find divergences emerging in domestically influenced categories, reflecting differences in national policies and institutional structures. A comparison of newspaper and Twitter discourse reveals that both media converge around a shared core during the pandemic, yet differ in intensity and temporal dynamics. Newspapers exhibit more stable political content, while Twitter reacts through short-lived event-driven spikes. These findings indicate that international crises act as a powerful synchronizing force on political content in classical media, temporarily overriding both national and media-form differences. Our automated political analysis empowers citizens by rendering political agendas in online media transparent. This transparency also enables media outlets to bridge the gap between algorithm-driven echo chambers and a more informed, balanced public discourse.

</details>


### [75] [Multimedia Asset Personalization via Multimodal Embeddings at Netflix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18322)
*Emma Yanyang Kong,Aditya Deshpande,Bowei Yan,Asad Abbasi,Santiago Castro,Avneesh Saluja,David Fagnan,Ashish Rastogi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Netflix 利用多模态嵌入（CLIP、自研 MediaFM）重塑个性化推广素材选择系统：单模型服务所有画布类型并改进冷启动，三模态视频预览模型超越单模态基线，离线代理任务加速实验迭代，分享生产工程落地经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于 ID 的交互历史模型无法理解素材内容，导致无法服务新上线标题和素材，且需为每种画布单独训练模型，冷启动表现差；需要利用基础模型嵌入实现跨标题、跨画布知识迁移，并融合多模态信号提升视频预览个性化效果。

Method: 1) 使用预训练 CLIP 图像嵌入增强双塔模型，单一模型服务五种画布类型，替代五个独立模型；利用 CLIP 联合文本-图像空间实现查询感知的艺术图个性化。2) 自研三模态基础模型 MediaFM，融合视觉、音频、时序文本信号，用于视频预览个性化。3) 设计离线代理任务：仅用嵌入预测基于流行度的胜者，作为嵌入模型版本的筛选门控，加速实验周期。同时构建共享嵌入基础设施、低延迟服务、廉价筛选机制。

Result: 单模型服务所有画布类型，显著提升冷启动性能；MediaFM 在离线和在线 A/B 测试中均超越强视觉基线；离线代理任务性能与在线结果相关，已作为每个 MediaFM 检查点的门控；生产工程决策使部署可行。

Conclusion: 多模态嵌入能有效解决推荐系统中的冷启动和内容理解问题，预训练基础模型（如 CLIP）可实现跨任务知识迁移；自研领域特定多模态模型（MediaFM）融合多模态信号显著提升性能；离线代理任务能加速模型迭代和产品化；生产工程决策（共享基础设施、低延迟、廉价筛选）对落地至关重要；同时也揭示了设计权衡和失效模式，为实践者提供可迁移的经验。

Abstract: Personalized promotional assets, namely artwork images and video preview clips, are critical to content discovery on Netflix. Traditional models for asset selection rely on ID-based interaction history, leaving them blind to asset content and unable to serve newly launched titles and assets. We describe how multimodal embeddings reshaped production systems at Netflix and report transferable lessons for practitioners adopting foundation-model embeddings into recommender systems. First, pretrained image embeddings unlock cross-title, cross-canvas knowledge transfer. Augmenting a two-tower model with CLIP image embeddings lets a single model serve all five Netflix artwork canvas types, replacing five separately trained per-canvas models and substantially improving cold-start performance. A lightweight extension reuses CLIP's joint text-image space to make artwork personalization query-aware in search. Second, multimodality decisively beats any single modality for video preview personalization. We describe MediaFM, our in-house tri-modal foundation model trained on a large-scale corpus of shots from the Netflix show catalog, fusing visual (SeqCLIP), audio (wav2vec 2.0), and timed-text signals; adopted for video preview personalization, it outperforms strong visual-only baselines both offline and in online A/B tests. Third, a simple offline proxy task whose performance correlates with online outcomes can accelerate the experimentation and productization cycle. Predicting the popularity-based winner from embeddings alone ranks embedding models and versions, pruning the choice space before any end-to-end integration or A/B test; it now gates every new MediaFM checkpoint. We also share the production engineering decisions (shared embedding infrastructure, low-latency serving, cheap screening) that made these deployments viable, along with the design tradeoffs and failure modes we encountered.

</details>


### [76] [AI in Search Reduces Publisher Referrals Without Improving User Experience: Experimental Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18352)
*Stephanie T. Wang,Jeffrey Gleason,Yakov Bart,Christo Wilson,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过在 Google Search 上的预注册田野实验（N=1,100），研究生成式 AI 概览（AI Overviews）和 AI 模式（AI Mode）对用户行为、感知及发布商流量的因果影响。结果显示：移除 AI 概览和 AI 模式能提高发布商点击率；仅提供 AI 模式则降低点击率，并损害用户体验与对 Google 信息的信任。这表明生成式 AI 融入网页搜索重塑了在线注意力分配，对维持搜索平台和整个信息生态的在线发布商产生经济后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式 AI 融入网页搜索提供合成答案，改变用户导航和评估信息的方式，但也引发对依赖底层内容的发布商下游影响的担忧。需要实证因果证据来量化 AI 概览和 AI 模式对用户行为、感知及发布商流量的影响。

Method: 在 Google Search 上进行预注册田野实验（N=1,100），通过操纵 AI 概览和 AI 模式的呈现条件，估计其对点击率、用户体验、信任度等指标的因果效应。

Result: 移除 AI 概览和 AI 模式显著增加发布商点击率；仅提供 AI 模式显著降低点击率，并侵蚀用户体验和对 Google 搜索结果的信任。

Conclusion: 生成式 AI 融入网页搜索重塑在线注意力分配，对维持搜索平台和整体信息生态的在线发布商产生实质性经济后果。政策制定者和平台需权衡 AI 增强搜索的便利性与发布商生存的可持续性。

Abstract: The integration of generative AI into web search delivers synthesized answers to user queries, changing how people navigate and assess information, while raising concerns about the downstream impacts on publishers who supply the underlying content. We conduct a preregistered field experiment (N=1,100) on Google Search, the dominant online search platform, to estimate the causal effects of AI Overviews and AI Mode on user behavior, perceptions, and publisher traffic. We show that removing AI Overviews and AI Mode increases click-through rates to publishers, while an AI Mode-only experience reduces click-through rates and erodes user experience and trust in information found on Google. These findings show that integrating generative AI into web search reshapes online attention, with economic consequences for the online publishers that sustain both search platforms and the overall information ecosystem.

</details>


### [77] [More Context, Same Budget: Dual-Bounded Relational Recall Beyond Top-K Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18448)
*Thomson D. Nguy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出双边界关系召回(DBRR)方法,在固定检索预算下通过关系图分配上下文,显著提升HotpotQA上完整证据集恢复率,较平铺top-k基线提升23.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平铺top-k检索忽略证据间关系,导致固定预算下无法完整恢复多跳问题所需证据集,需利用证据间关系的检索分配策略。

Method: 提出Dual-Bounded Relational Recall (DBRR),固定检索预算(最大检索单元数和token数)下,将预算分配给相关性选中的种子节点和有界图邻居上下文,在HotpotQA FullWiki 7,405问题上与匹配的平铺top-k基线配对比较,并以随机邻居和度保持打乱图为对照。

Result: DBRR使完整支持证据恢复率提升23.8百分点(配对风险差0.2377, 95% CI 0.2269-0.2489),改进1952问题,持平5261,损害192。桥接问题提升28.7点,比较问题提升4.2点。真实关系优于随机邻居和打乱图对照。

Conclusion: 相同上下文预算下,完整证据检索不仅取决于排名靠前项目,还取决于如何围绕它们分配上下文。关系分配恢复了平铺top-k遗漏的完整证据集。

Abstract: More context does not require a larger retrieval budget. Under the same ceiling, a retrieval system can recover more of the evidence a question requires by following relationships between evidence that flat top-k ranking leaves behind. We test that proposition with Dual-Bounded Relational Recall (DBRR), which allocates a fixed retrieval budget between relevance-selected seeds and bounded graph-adjacent context, against matched flat top-k retrieval using the same relevance-ranking stage and the same maximum number of retrieval units and tokens. The outcome is complete recovery of the official HotpotQA supporting-evidence set for each question.
  Across 7,405 FullWiki questions, the Primary DBRR allocation increased complete supporting-evidence recovery by 23.8 percentage points over its matched flat baseline (paired risk difference 0.2377; question-level bootstrap 95% interval 0.2269 to 0.2489). It improved 1,952 questions, tied on 5,261, and harmed 192. Bridge questions drove the effect, with a 28.7-point increase; comparison questions showed a smaller 4.2-point difference. In a prespecified, evaluation-only diagnostic population, real relationships also outperformed random-neighbor and degree-preserving shuffled-graph controls.
  The result is straightforward: under the same context budget, complete-evidence retrieval depends not only on which items rank highest, but on how context is allocated around them. Relational allocation recovered complete evidence sets that flat top-k retrieval left incomplete.

</details>


### [78] [OneModel: A Unified Foundation for Platform-Scale Multi-Scenario Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18606)
*Yinqi Zhang,Peiyu Hu,Yuntian Tang,Siying Gu,Jiahao Liang,Longxin Kou,Haiqing Hu,Shuman Zhuang,Yubin Xu,Chenggen Sun,Bin Ye,Donghui Xu,Zhaoyu Liu,Jiang Rong,Yuting Jia,Zhaokai Luo,Leilei Ma,Yiying Xie,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OneModel是小红书部署的统一多流最终排序框架，通过共享事件序列、长上下文用户表示、场景感知信息调制及生产级优化，在探索流、广告流、商家流三大业务场景均实现显著在线指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台级推荐系统跨越自然推荐、广告、商家服务等多业务流，用户行为形成连续跨流轨迹。维护独立排序系统导致用户表示碎片化、工程成本高昂，亟需统一框架实现跨流迁移与流内专业化的平衡。

Method: OneModel将异构行为映射为共享事件序列，采用动作导向骨干网络学习长上下文用户表示，引入场景感知信息调制机制平衡跨流迁移与流内专业化。生产部署层面，采用分层用户表示、多目标训练，以及特征分解、用户特征预取、共享用户塔计算、图级推理优化等在线服务优化。

Result: 离线实验超越强基线，随上下文长度和模型容量良性扩展。在线A/B测试：探索流人均时长+0.33%、互动率+1.25%；Feed广告广告价值+3.43%、CTR+8.18%；商家推荐DGMV+1.1867%、GPM+2.1585%。

Conclusion: 统一多流排序作为生产基础设施行之有效，OneModel验证了单一模型跨业务流联合优化的可行性与优势，为大规模推荐系统架构演进提供了实践范式。

Abstract: Platform-scale recommender systems often span multiple business streams such as organic recommendation, advertising, and merchant services, where user behaviors form a continuous cross-stream trajectory. Maintaining separate ranking systems fragments user representations and increases engineering cost. We propose \textbf{OneModel}, a unified framework for multi-stream final ranking. OneModel maps heterogeneous behaviors into shared event sequences, learns long-context user representations with an action-oriented backbone, and introduces \emph{Scenario-aware Information Modulation} to balance cross-stream transfer and stream-specific specialization. For production deployment, OneModel further adopts stratified user representation, multi-objective training, and optimized online serving with feature decomposition, user feature prefetching, shared user-tower computation, and graph-level inference optimization. We deploy OneModel in production at \emph{Xiaohongshu}, where it delivers consistent offline gains over strong baselines and scales favorably with context length and model capacity. Online A/B tests improve Time Spent by \textbf{+0.33\%} and Engagement by \textbf{+1.25\%} in Explore Feed, lift advertising value by \textbf{+3.43\%} and CTR by \textbf{+8.18\%} in Feed Advertising, and raise DGMV by \textbf{+1.1867\%} and GPM by \textbf{+2.1585\%} in Merchant Recommendation, validating unified multi-stream ranking as an effective production foundation.

</details>


### [79] [PILOT Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18637)
*Jiuning Lin,Ruiquan Lan,Xiaodong Zhu,Bin Zhang,Chengyu Lai,Chuxin Chen,Dimin Wang,Hongtao Cheng,Jialin Zhu,Lingqing Zhang,Shuai Zhong,Tao Wang,Weipeng Huang,Yinjiang Cai,Yinnan Song,Yuan Liu,Zhibo Xiao,Zhixin Ma,Zihong Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PILOT 是一个基于 LLM 的主动式实验代理框架，通过实验管理器、搜索规划器和记忆策展人三角色协作，在淘宝平台实现用户分段级个性化实验，显著提升 IPV、交易量等指标及搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统优化代理方法本质上是被动的：仅响应指标变化调整参数，缺乏主动设计受控实验、用户分段级个性化策略及跨任务复用实验方法论的能力。

Method: PILOT 框架包含三个角色在受约束控制循环中协作：(1) 实验管理器驱动完整实验生命周期，仅从规则生成的合法命令集选择动作；(2) 搜索规划器在管理器请求时提出用户分段级决策树候选；(3) 记忆策展人异步提炼实验结果为策略级领域知识与溯源方法论，与主循环故障隔离。确定性服务强制执行安全、统计与权限边界。

Result: 在淘宝平台 5 个实验分桶部署，对比无生命周期治理的自由探索代理 ROAM，PILOT 实现 IPV +1.40%、核心 IPV +1.60%、交易数 +0.96%、交易额 +1.50%，优于 ROAM 最佳结果（+1.00% IPV, +0.90% 核心 IPV, +0.60% 交易数, +1.13% 交易额），搜索效率从 53.3% 提升至 93.3%（+40 个百分点），全程无人工干预。

Conclusion: PILOT 通过主动式实验管理、分段级个性化规划与异步知识沉淀，解决了推荐优化中被动调参、缺乏结构化假设检验与方法论复用的问题，在工业级部署中显著提升业务指标与实验效率，验证了 LLM 代理在受约束环境下进行主动实验设计的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Existing agentic approaches for recommendation system optimization remain fundamentally reactive: they adjust parameters in response to observed metric changes but lack the ability to proactively design controlled experiments, personalize strategies at the user-segment level, or accumulate reusable experimental methodology across tasks. We present PILOT (Proactive Insight Learner for Online Tree-Experiments), an LLM-agent framework that organizes three roles within a constrained control loop where deterministic services enforce all safety, statistical, and permission boundaries: (1) an Experiment Manager that drives the full experiment lifecycle -- task intake, observation governance, anomaly recovery, and postmortem -- by selecting only from a rule-generated legal-command envelope; (2) a Search Planner that proposes candidate decision trees for user-segment-level personalization, invoked only when the Manager requests planning; and (3) a Memory Curator that asynchronously distills experiment outcomes into strategy-level domain knowledge and provenance-tracked methodology, failure-isolated from the main loop. The Manager makes the agent proactive, the Planner enables population-level personalization beyond global tuning, and the Curator turns every completed task into a learning opportunity for the next. Deployed on Taobao's platform with 5 experimental buckets, PILOT is compared against ROAM(Reactive Optimization with Agent-driven Moves), a free-exploration agent without lifecycle governance or structured hypothesis testing. PILOT achieves up to +1.40% IPV, +1.60% Core IPV, +0.96% transaction count, and +1.50% transaction amount, improving over ROAM's best results (+1.00% IPV, +0.90% Core IPV, +0.60% transaction count, +1.13% transaction amount) while raising search efficiency from 53.3% to 93.3% (+40 pp), with no human intervention throughout the experimental cycle.

</details>


### [80] [GreekBarRetrieval: A Benchmark for Greek Statutory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18752)
*Ernest Beta,Odysseas S. Chlapanis,Dimitrios Galanis,Ion Androutsopoulos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文针对希腊语法律领域的法条检索任务，构建了首个公开基准 GreekBarRetrieval，并系统评估了稀疏检索（BM25 变体）与密集检索器的表现。实验发现：原始密集检索在 Recall@100 上显著优于稀疏检索；引入基于 LLM 的查询重述（尤其是十轮 ReAct-like 循环）能大幅缩小差距，并使 BM25 在 nDCG 和 MAP 指标上超越所有密集检索器。查询重述效果优于伪相关反馈、稀疏-密集融合及英语翻译等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律问答需要引用法条作为依据，但希腊语的法条检索研究严重不足。现有的 GreekBarBench 仅包含问答，缺乏检索环节。日常语言表述的案件事实与法条的正式术语、抽象法律概念存在鸿沟，且案件事实中并非所有内容都与特定问题相关，增加了检索难度。因此亟需构建专用基准并探索有效的检索增强方法。

Method: 1. 构建 GreekBarRetrieval 基准：包含 283 道律师资格考试题目、对应的案件事实，以及 6,308 条候选法条。2. 实验对比三种 BM25 变体与九个密集检索器的零样本表现。3. 提出基于 LLM 的查询重述策略，包括单轮重述与十轮 ReAct-like 循环重述（模拟推理-行动-观察循环）。4. 将查询重述与伪相关反馈、稀疏-密集融合、英语翻译等基线进行对比。

Result: 零样本设置下，密集检索在 Recall@100 上远超稀疏检索。LLM 查询重述显著提升 BM25 召回率，缩小与密集检索的差距，同时也改善密集检索效果。十轮 ReAct-like 循环重述使 BM25 在 Recall@100 上进一步提升，并在 nDCG 和 MAP 上取得所有检索器中的最优分数。查询重述全面优于伪相关反馈、稀疏-密集融合及英语翻译方案。

Conclusion: 研究填补了希腊语法条检索的基准空白，证明查询重述——特别是多轮 ReAct-like 循环重述——是提升稀疏检索性能的关键，甚至能使 BM25 在排序质量指标上超越强大的密集检索器。该方法无需额外训练数据，具有较强的实用性和泛化潜力，为低资源语言法律检索提供了可复用的范式。

Abstract: Statutory retrieval is necessary for citation-grounded legal question answering, but remains underexplored for Greek. We introduce GreekBarRetrieval, a public retrieval benchmark derived from, and complementing GreekBarBench, which did not include retrieval. The new benchmark comprises 283 bar-exam questions, each accompanied by the facts of the case it refers to, and 6,308 candidate statutory articles to retrieve from. Questions and facts are stated in everyday language, but need to be mapped to the formal terminology of statutes and their abstract legal concepts. A further complication is that not all of the case facts are relevant to each question of a case. Experimenting with three BM25 variants and nine dense retrievers, we find that vanilla dense retrieval far outperforms vanilla sparse retrieval in Recall@100. However, LLM-based query reformulation helps BM25 close that gap, while also improving dense retrieval. With a ten-round ReAct-like LLM reformulation loop that we introduce, BM25 improves further in Recall@100 and obtains the best nDCG and MAP scores of all tested retrievers. Query reformulation also outperforms pseudo-relevance feedback, sparse-dense fusion, and English translation.

</details>


### [81] [GateDiffInt: Gate-Mediated Controllable Diffusion and Multi-Intent LLM Distillation for User Behavior Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18764)
*Jialong Duan,Zichen Zhang,Zirui Tu,Zheng Zhang,Zepeng Li,Qingyao Cui,Qinwen Wang,Yudan Liu,Luo Yang,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出GateDiffInt框架，通过扩散过程与双门控机制联合去噪与意图提取，利用大模型蒸馏四类结构化意图，经注意力融合后用于转化率预测，在工业大规模数据上显著提升GMV并已上线主流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排序模型仅隐式编码意图，难以解耦不同强度和时间尺度的结构化意图。行为序列中的噪声与意图相互耦合（NIC）：噪声稀释真实意图，而缺乏结构化意图先验导致去噪无明确目标。

Method: 1. 利用最终转化信号联合对齐序列去噪与意图提取；2. 采用可控前向扩散过程配合双门控机制增强并去噪行为序列；3. 引入大语言模型作为教师模型，将长期、短期、潜在、转化四类结构化意图蒸馏至轻量学生模型；4. 通过注意力机制深度融合增强序列与结构化意图表示，生成意图感知表示用于转化率预测。

Result: 在公开及大规模工业数据集上持续超越强基线；在服务数亿日活用户的在线A/B测试中实现显著GMV提升，并已部署至主流量，验证了有效性与生产就绪性。

Conclusion: GateDiffInt通过显式建模结构化意图并联合去噪，有效解决了噪声-意图耦合问题，为工业排序提供了可落地的意图交互范式，显著提升了转化率预测精度与业务指标。

Abstract: Existing ranking models encode intent only implicitly, making it hard to disentangle structured intents of varying strength and temporal scale. Noise and intent in behavior sequences are mutually reinforcing---we call this Noise--Intent Coupling (NIC). Noise dilutes true intents, while the lack of structured intent priors leaves denoising without a clear target.To address NIC, we propose GateDiffInt, an intent interaction framework for industrial ranking. It uses the final conversion signal to jointly align sequence denoising and intent extraction. GateDiffInt applies a controllable forward diffusion process with dual gating to enhance and denoise behavior sequences. A large language model then acts as teacher to distill four structured intents---long-term, short-term, latent, and conversion---into a lightweight student model. The enhanced sequence and structured intent representations are deeply fused via attention to produce intent-aware representations for conversion-rate prediction.Extensive experiments on public and large-scale industrial datasets show consistent gains over strong baselines. In online A/B tests serving hundreds of millions of daily active users, GateDiffInt delivers substantial GMV improvements and has been deployed to primary traffic, confirming both effectiveness and production readiness.

</details>


### [82] [SIDScope: A Diagnostic Resource for Semantic-ID Interfaces in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18779)
*Jiandong Ding,Huijie Qin,Tiandeng Wu,Yi Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIDScope是一个用于诊断语义ID映射的源码追踪工具，揭示了接口健康是多信号而非单一标量，发现前缀对齐在检索消费SID前缀时强相关候选暴露，但在评分变为前缀无关时减弱；追踪核算显示有效目标路径可能无法唯一检索目标项（1.2-3.0个百分点）；修复映射不足以恢复继承生成器，模型复用需单独交接检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义ID映射发布缺乏连贯性声明、结构暴露、路径解析说明及刷新后重新验证指南，导致难以评估接口健康和复用风险。

Method: SIDScope规范化项目到代码工件，验证来源和连接，剖析映射结构，比较配对修订，并在生成追踪中核算路径到项目结果。在七个分词器家族的九个源码追踪导出上进行实验（Amazon和Yelp数据，八个可执行路线加一个可审计快照）。

Result: 发现接口健康是多信号而非标量；核心发现随机制条件变化：前缀对齐在基于前缀检索时强相关举出候选暴露，随评分变为前缀无关而减弱；追踪核算暴露第二隐藏缺口：有效目标路径可能以1.2-3.0个百分点无法唯一检索目标项；刷新案例确立第三发现：修复映射本身不足以恢复继承生成器，模型复用需要单独交接检查。

Conclusion: SIDScope提供冻结证据摘要、一致性报告、追踪标签、表格构建器和纯CPU验证器，支持工件就绪、接口风险及模型复用前的重新验证决策。

Abstract: Semantic-ID mappings are reusable interfaces between item tokenizers and generative recommenders, yet released mappings rarely state whether they are coherent, what structure they expose, how generated paths resolve, or what must be revalidated after a refresh. SIDScope is a source-traced diagnostic resource for these decisions. It normalizes item-to-code artifacts, verifies provenance and joins, profiles mapping structure, compares paired revisions, and accounts for path-to-item outcomes in generated traces. Across nine source-traced tokenizer exports from seven families on Amazon and Yelp data - eight executable routes plus one auditable snapshot - SIDScope reveals that interface health is multi-signal rather than scalar. Its central finding is mechanism-conditional: prefix alignment strongly tracks held-out candidate exposure when retrieval consumes SID prefixes, then weakens as scoring becomes prefix-independent. Trained trace accounting exposes a second hidden gap: a valid target path can survive without uniquely retrieving the target item by 1.2-3.0 percentage points. A refresh case establishes a third: repairing the mapping does not by itself restore an inherited generator; model reuse requires a separate handoff check. The package provides frozen evidence summaries, conformance reports, trace labels, table builders, and CPU-only verifiers. It supports decisions about artifact readiness, interface risks, and revalidation before model reuse.

</details>


### [83] [Think-to-Personalize: Unifying Reasoning and Retrieval for User-Centric Personalized Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18855)
*Angqing Jiang,Gaoming Zhang,Jianchun Song,Kena Qi,Dayao Chen,Wei Lin,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Think-to-Personalize (TTP) 框架，通过大语言模型对用户历史购买序列进行显式意图推理，生成意图增强查询并融入稠密检索，采用 SFT 冷启动 + GRPO 强化学习两阶段训练，在专有/公开基准及线上 A/B 测试中显著超越现有方法，订单量提升 0.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商稠密检索将 LLM 仅作为静态编码器，忽视其推理能力；且以查询为中心难以弥补稀疏/模糊查询导致的意图鸿沟；现有个性化检索依赖隐式嵌入交互，缺乏从噪声历史行为中消歧用户意图的推理能力。

Method: TTP 框架：(1) 对用户历史购买序列进行显式推理，推导潜在个性化需求并生成意图增强查询；(2) 将增强查询编码为统一稠密嵌入用于检索；(3) 两阶段训练：SFT 建立冷启动推理能力，RL 阶段用 GRPO 将推理过程与检索效用对齐。

Result: 在专有数据集和公开基准上显著优于 SOTA 基线；线上 A/B 测试实现订单量 +0.46% 提升，验证了实用有效性。

Conclusion: TTP 建立了推理驱动的个性化稠密检索新范式，证明显式意图推理与强化学习对齐能有效解决电商搜索中的意图鸿沟与历史行为噪声问题。

Abstract: Dense retrieval has become a cornerstone of modern local-lifestyle e-commerce search by encoding queries and items into semantic embedding spaces. While recent advancements have transitioned from BERT-based embedding models to Large Language Models (LLMs), most approaches still treat LLMs as static text encoders, neglecting their inherent reasoning capabilities. Furthermore, standard dense retrieval models remain query-centric, which is insufficient in e-commerce scenarios where sparse and ambiguous queries create an intent gap that can only be bridged by the rich context of user history. Meanwhile, existing personalized retrieval methods typically rely on implicit embedding interactions, which lack the reasoning capability to effectively disambiguate user intent from noisy historical behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose Think-to-Personalize (TTP), a novel framework that unifies explicit user-centric intent reasoning with dense retrieval. By reasoning over the user's historical purchase sequence, TTP explicitly deduces latent personalized needs and generates an intent-enhanced query, which is then encoded into a unified dense embedding. Specifically, we design a two-stage training paradigm: (1) a Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) stage that establishes cold-start capabilities; and (2) a Reinforcement Learning (RL) stage that aligns the reasoning process with retrieval utility using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Extensive experiments on both proprietary and public benchmarks demonstrate that TTP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, in online A/B tests, it achieved a +0.46% lift in order volume, validating its practical effectiveness and establishing a new paradigm for reasoning-driven personalized dense retrieval.

</details>


### [84] [rEDMRec: Distilling Large Language Model Reasoning into an Editable Experience Memory for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18952)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen,Tung Le,Huy Tien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 rEDMRec，将教师大语言模型（LLM）的推理蒸馏为四类可编辑的结构化记忆通道（长期偏好、短期上下文、物品感知、反事实难负例对比），由 LLM 记忆控制器通过增删改保操作和 K-agent 辩论维护，轻量级学生模型仅通过检索记忆进行排序，实现在线推理成本与推理深度解耦，在多个数据集和学生骨干上显著提升推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推荐方法在每次排序请求时重复进行昂贵的显式推理，且推理结果一次性使用后丢弃，无法跨请求复用，也难以在用户兴趣漂移时进行检查或修正。

Method: rEDMRec 包含三个核心组件：1) 四类类型化、可编辑的经验通道：长期偏好、短期上下文、物品感知、反事实难负例对比。2) LLM 记忆控制器：对通道条目执行 Add/Delete/Modify/Keep 操作，并通过 K-agent 辩论优化条目质量。3) 轻量级学生 LLM：仅从记忆库检索信息进行候选排序，无需再次调用教师模型。

Result: 在 ML-1M、Amazon Beauty、Steam 三个数据集及十个学生骨干上，rEDMRec 的 HR@1 均优于零样本、少样本、RAG 及大多数 GraphRAG 基线，ML-1M 上相对次优基线提升高达 13.3%。消融实验显示短期上下文通道在各容量层级均稳定有效，其他三通道贡献随学生模型容量变化（甚至在最强学生上反向）；基于辩论的记忆优化将库重复率降低 7.4 个百分点，并在六轮优化中使下游 HR@1 最高提升 +0.029。

Conclusion: 通过将 LLM 推理压缩为结构化、可维护的记忆库，rEDMRec 实现了推理复用、可检查性和低成本在线推理的统一，为大模型增强推荐系统提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Large language models can improve recommendation quality by reasoning explicitly over user history and candidate items - for example, extracting a user's preferences or explaining why one item fits better than another - rather than mapping history directly to a ranked list. This reasoning, however, is expensive to repeat on every ranking request and, once produced, is typically consumed once and discarded, leaving it neither reusable across future requests nor easy to inspect or correct as user tastes drift. Our insight is that reasoning does not need to be regenerated at every call if it can instead be compressed once into a compact, structured memory that a lightweight model retrieves from. We propose rEDMRec, which distills a teacher LLM's reasoning into four typed, editable experience channels - long-term preference, short-term context, item-perception, and counterfactual hard-negative comparisons - maintained by an LLM memory controller that performs Add/Delete/Modify/Keep operations and refines entries via K-agent debate. A lightweight student LLM then ranks candidates purely by retrieving from this memory, without invoking the teacher again, decoupling online inference cost from reasoning depth. Across ML-1M, Amazon Beauty, and Steam and ten student backbones, rEDMRec improves HR@1 over zero-shot, few-shot, and RAG on every backbone, and over GraphRAG on most backbones, with Impv up to 13.3% vs. the second-best baseline on ML-1M. Channel ablations show that short-term context is the only channel that helps consistently across capacity tiers, whereas long-term, item-perception, and counterfactual contributions are capacity-dependent (and can reverse on the strongest students); debate-based memory optimization lowers bank duplication by 7.4 percentage points while raising downstream HR@1 by up to +0.029 over six optimization epochs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [What Can Artificial Intelligence Learn from Medicine? Generative Analogies and Reliable Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18186)
*Emanuele Ratti,Lena Zuchowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨机器学习在医学中的应用，通过Hesse的类比理论，将临床转化的过程与构建ML系统的过程建立生成性类比，从可靠论视角重新诠释临床转化的认识论和方法论根据，提出一种新型的ML可靠论，区别于现有AI哲学中的可靠论观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在医学中广泛应用，但其认识论和方法论根据存在不确定性。文献中常将医学与ML类比，建议以临床转化标准作为ML标准的模型，但这一类比的具体性质和适用范围尚未被精确刻画。

Method: 运用Hesse的类比理论工具，将临床转化过程与ML系统构建过程建立为生成性类比；识别临床转化中通常仅在类比中被提及的认识论和方法论根据；以可靠论框架重新诠释这些根据，并展示其如何类比性地应用于ML语境。

Result: 精确刻画了临床转化的认识论和方法论根据，证明这些根据可通过类比方式应用于ML；提出一种新型ML可靠论，该理论与现有AI哲学中的可靠论观点既有区别又兼容。

Conclusion: 通过生成性类比和可靠论重构，为ML在医学中的应用提供了更坚实的认识论基础，新型ML可靠论为解决ML认识论不确定性提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: In the past few years, machine learning (ML) has been widely (and to an extent, successfully) implemented in medicine. However, uncertainties surrounding ML have made it difficult to establish the bases of its epistemic and methodological warrants. In the literature, a parallel has been drawn between medicine and ML, suggesting that we should model epistemic and methodological standards for ML on the standards of clinical translation. By developing tools from Hesse work, we characterise the nature of this parallel as a generative analogy between the process of clinical translation and the process of building ML systems. We identify more precisely the epistemic and methodological warrants of clinical translation that are typically only mentioned when appealing to the analogy, and we show in which sense such warrants apply analogically to the context of ML. In particular, we interpret warrants of clinical translation in reliabilist terms, and we show how this can inform a new form of ML reliabilism, which is distinct from (though compatible with) existing reliabilist accounts in philosophy of AI.

</details>


### [86] [A systematic review of machine learning techniques to address diagnosis and treatment of autism: challenges and opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18188)
*Rafael Muñoz-Terol,Jesús Peral,Sandra Amador,David Gil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该系统性综述分析了2017-2023年间55项关于机器学习在自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）研究中应用的研究，旨在识别趋势、技术和数据集以提升诊断和治疗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD病因不明，早期诊断需要识别相关特征和隐性关联。机器学习技术可辅助诊断，但需系统梳理当前应用现状、挑战与机遇，指导未来研究方向。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，纳入2017-2023年发表的55项研究，分析机器学习技术（以监督学习为主、深度学习逐渐扩展、混合方法新兴）在ASD诊断与治疗中的应用趋势、常用数据集及技术特点。

Result: 监督学习主导，深度学习随数据增加而扩展；混合方法（无监督、深度学习、模糊逻辑）为新兴方向。关键挑战包括：需整合遗传、临床等复杂多模态数据以提高诊断准确性；可穿戴设备和生物传感器等创新数据源可实现持续无创监测。研究强调跨学科合作和扩展针对ASD的数据集的必要性。

Conclusion: 未来机器学习模型将受益于更广泛的多模态数据整合，从而更全面地应对ASD的复杂性，提升诊断准确性和治疗效果。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) is a developmental disability characterized by challenges in social interaction and communication. As the causes of ASD remain unclear, identifying relevant features and hidden correlations is crucial for early diagnosis. This systematic review evaluates 55 studies from 2017 to 2023 on the application of machine learning (ML) techniques to ASD. The primary objective is to examine recent ML applications in ASD research, identifying trends, techniques, and datasets that enhance diagnosis and treatment. Supervised learning methods dominate, as they align well with ASD diagnostic needs; however, the role of deep learning is expanding with greater data availability. Emerging techniques based on hybrid methods, where unsupervised, deep learning, and fuzzy logic could be included, will be interesting to observe in the future. The review highlights key challenges and opportunities, particularly the need for models that can integrate complex data -such as genetic and clinical information- to improve diagnostic accuracy and treatment outcomes. Additionally, incorporating innovative data sources, like wearable devices and biometric sensors, could enable continuous and non-intrusive monitoring, providing a more holistic understanding of ASD. Findings emphasize that addressing current challenges requires interdisciplinary collaboration and expanded datasets tailored to ASD. Future ML models will benefit from broader multimodal data integration, enabling researchers to more comprehensively address the complexities of ASD.

</details>


### [87] [Safe Domain Adaptation for Physics: Overcoming Nuisances, Label Shifts, and Simulation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18190)
*Ivan Kharuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对物理实验中领域自适应的两个关键假设失效问题：模拟与实验不仅存在干扰差异，且目标量（如能谱）分布本身不同。提出自适应领域自适应方法，通过重加权模拟事件聚焦真实物理错配，并给出无标签模型选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理领域中，标准领域自适应假设两域仅在干扰因素上不同且目标量分布相同，但实际中模拟可能存在物理错误，且目标量分布（如能谱）往往是测量目标本身，导致标准方法失效。

Method: 提出自适应领域自适应，通过重加权模拟事件使其目标量分布与实验一致，从而将领域自适应聚焦于真实的物理错配；并设计无标签模型选择规则，根据训练配置选择近似最优操作点。

Result: 在玩具空气簇射基准上实验表明，标准对抗自适应能处理条件分布偏移，但当能谱不同时会强行对齐谱形，引入基于模拟先验的偏差；自适应方法有效分离干扰与物理错配，提升校准精度。

Conclusion: 自适应领域自适应通过重加权解决目标量分布不匹配问题，结合无标签模型选择，为物理实验中基于模拟的神经网络校准提供了更鲁棒的框架。

Abstract: Domain adaptation is widely used to make neural networks trained on simulations applicable to experimental data. Its premise is that the two domains differ only in nuisances, and that the quantity of interest is distributed identically in both. In physics neither assumption holds: simulations can be wrong about the physics, and the distribution of the target quantity - an energy spectrum, a redshift distribution - is often the measurement itself. We study the consequences of such mismatches on a toy air-shower benchmark in which a detector-response nuisance, a physical simulation shift, and an energy-spectrum shift can be switched on separately or together. Standard adversarial adaptation handles the conditional shifts, but once the two spectra differ it aligns them, replacing an uncontrolled bias by one anchored on the simulation prior. We present adaptive domain adaptation, which reweights the simulated events so as to focus domain adaptation on the genuine physical mismatch alone. Since the predicted spectrum depends on model training configuration, we provide a label-free model selection rule for selecting the near-the-best operation point.

</details>


### [88] [ChiroEcho: extending automated bat vocalisation classification beyond the learned taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18191)
*Burooj Ghani,Welmoed Eversteijn,Milan van Hirtum,Juan Sebastián Cañas,Vincent J. Kalkman,Dan Stowell,A. Leonie Baier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度学习框架，联合预测蝙蝠物种和属，并结合地理分布信息在推理阶段扩展分类器的有效分类范围，将欧洲蝙蝠自动声学分类的操作覆盖率从 35 种提升至 41 种（占 48 种本土物种的 85%），实现了目前最广的覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝙蝠是生态系统健康的关键指示物种，全欧受保护，但其隐秘夜行习性使得被动声学监测必不可少；现有自动识别因回声定位叫声随行为和环境变化且物种间重叠而困难，且训练数据难以覆盖所有物种，亟需一种能利用地理信息推断未见物种的方法。

Method: 构建联合预测物种和属的深度学习模型，在推理时将属级预测与该地区的物种地理分布数据结合：若某属在该地区仅分布单一物种，则可直接推断该物种标签，从而将地理信息作为扩展而非约束分类器分类空间的手段。

Result: 在 35 种欧洲蝙蝠的录音上评估闭集分类，分析稀有物种性能估计的不稳定性，并进行受控的留出实验。结果显示：地理分辨将操作覆盖从 35 种扩展至 41 种（覆盖率 73%→85%），属预测加位置信息能恢复物种头未见的标签，为精细粒度未见类别的识别提供了概念验证。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了将粗粒度预测与透明外部约束（地理分布）相结合，可有效解决细粒度分类中未见类别的识别问题，显著提升了欧洲蝙蝠声学监测的物种覆盖范围，为生物声学自动分类提供了可推广的范式。

Abstract: Bats are key indicators of ecosystem health and are protected throughout Europe, making reliable population monitoring a conservation priority. Their cryptic nocturnal lifestyle makes passive acoustic monitoring essential, yet automated identification remains difficult as echolocation calls vary with behaviour and environment and overlap among species. We present a deep learning framework that jointly predicts species and genus and combines genus predictions with geographic species distributions at inference. When only one species of a predicted genus occurs in a region, the framework can resolve species absent from the learned taxonomy. This reframes geographic information as a means of extending, rather than constraining, a classifier's effective taxonomy. Using recordings spanning 35 European bat species, we evaluate closed-set classification, examine the instability of performance estimates for sparsely represented species, and conduct a controlled held-out proof-of-principle experiment. The rare-species analysis shows how limited evaluation data can obscure species-level performance, while the held-out experiment shows that genus predictions and location can recover labels unavailable to the species head. Geographic resolution extends operational coverage from 35 to 41 of the 48 native European bat species, increasing coverage from 73% to 85%. To our knowledge, this is the broadest operational coverage reported for automated European bat classification. More broadly, the bat framework provides proof of principle for resolving unseen fine-grained classes by combining coarse predictions with transparent external constraints.

</details>


### [89] [Think Shallow, Solve Deep: Controlling Recurrent Dynamics for Reliable Test-Time Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18222)
*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Amirah Almutairi,Serguei Barannikov,Maxim Abramov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究循环深度推理器在测试时增加迭代步数的效果，提出有限时间动力学机制（收敛、临界、发散）可预测额外迭代是提升、保持还是降低答案质量；给出深度安全充分条件：算子单步位移小于解码器边界时答案不再变化。实验表明收敛型算子不随深度增加而退化，甚至能在更难的未见实例上提升准确率（如数独从0.19提至0.34）；终端不动点目标能统一调节动力学机制与深度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环深度推理器在测试时通过增加迭代次数解决更难问题，但额外迭代可能改善、保持或损害答案，缺乏理论指标预测这种行为，也缺乏保证深度安全的条件。

Method: 1. 定义有限时间动力学机制（收敛/临界/发散）表征训练算子行为；2. 提出深度安全充分条件：单步位移小于解码器边界；3. 在算法任务上从每难度800个未增广样本训练，实证验证；4. 分析终端不动点目标对动力学机制的影响；5. 提出四个操作性判据目录化失效模式；6. 将测量方法应用于Huginn-3.5B进行一致性检验。

Result: 收敛型算子随深度增加不退化，部分任务（数独）在超出训练分布的更难实例上准确率从0.19提升至0.34；移除终端不动点目标导致发散并消除增益，添加该目标使通用循环在进位传播任务上实现深度安全外推；Huginn-3.5B属于非收敛家族，验证了测量方法的通用性。

Conclusion: 有限时间动力学机制是预测测试时深度行为的关键指标，深度安全可由单步位移与解码器边界的关系保证；终端不动点目标能协同调节动力学机制与深度外推能力，为设计可靠的循环推理器提供了理论依据和实用判据。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth reasoners aim to solve harder problems by iterating their update longer at test time, but additional iterations can improve, preserve, or degrade an answer. We show that a measurable property of the trained operator, its finite-time dynamical regime (estimated as settling, marginal, or drifting), indicates which of these occurs. We give a sufficient condition for depth-safety: once an operator's per-step displacement is small relative to the decoder margin, the decoded answer cannot change under further iterations. Empirically, on algorithmic tasks trained from $800$ unaugmented examples per difficulty tier, settling operators do not degrade with added depth, and on some tasks convert it into higher accuracy on harder unseen instances (Sudoku, $0.19$ to $0.34$ past the training horizon). A single terminal fixed-point objective moves the regime and the depth behavior together: removing it induces drift and removes the gains, and adding it to a generic recurrence yields depth-safe extrapolation on carry propagation. We give four operational criteria for useful test-time depth, use them to catalogue failure modes, and, as a consistency check, apply the same measurements to Huginn-3.5B, which falls in the non-settling family.

</details>


### [90] [Allocating Recurrent Compute in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18230)
*Ruhai Lin,Yiyang Guo,Rui-Jie Zhu,Hao Ye,Jason K. Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MixerLoop 架构，通过仅循环 Gated DeltaNet mixer 而非整个 Transformer 块（包括稠密 FFN），在保持循环深度收益的同时显著降低计算开销。实验表明，在 15M 参数下 MixerLoop 超越全块循环，在 110M 参数下保留 41.5% 的 CORE 提升，同时将循环骨干投影 FLOPs 降低 45.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型通常重复整个层栈，但 mixer 和稠密 FFN 执行不同操作且成本不同。本文旨在回答'什么应该循环'这一问题，从状态更新的重复组合视角分析循环价值，提出迭代传输秩（ITR）及边际 ITR 来量化跨位置影响方向的可观测性与非冗余贡献。

Method: 提出 MixerLoop：仅重复应用 Gated DeltaNet mixer，而稠密 FFN 仅应用一次。引入有限上下文切除干预实验，测试后续 mixer 应用是否在最终语言模型读出处产生独特、非微不足道且有益的变化。在相同数据、初始化和架构下，对比无循环、全块循环与 MixerLoop 在 15M 和 110M 参数规模下的表现。

Result: 15M 参数下 MixerLoop 在聚合 CORE 指标上超越 FullLoop；110M 参数下保留 FullLoop 41.5% 的 CORE 改进，同时将循环骨干投影 FLOPs 降低 45.9%。有限上下文切除实验验证了后续 mixer 应用确实产生了可观测的、非冗余的有益影响。

Conclusion: 循环深度的收益无需重复执行稠密 FFN 即可保留。通过仅循环 mixer 组件，可大幅降低计算成本同时维持大部分性能提升，为高效循环架构设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Looped language models improve reasoning and knowledge manipulation by applying shared computation repeatedly. Existing systems usually repeat an entire layer stack, although a mixer and a dense feed-forward network (FFN) perform different operations and have different costs. We ask a narrower question: what should loop? We view recurrence as repeated composition of a state update and argue that an application is valuable when it exposes a new cross-position influence direction that remains observable at the task readout. Iterative Transport Rank (ITR) describes the cumulative influence trajectory; marginal ITR describes the nonredundant influence contributed by successive applications. This view motivates MixerLoop, which repeats each Gated DeltaNet mixer while applying its dense FFN once. We compare MixerLoop with no recurrence and full-block recurrence at 15M and 110M parameters under the same data, initialization, and architecture. A finite context-off intervention tests whether later mixer applications produce distinct, non-negligible, and beneficial changes at the final language-model readout. MixerLoop surpasses FullLoop on aggregate CORE at 15M and retains 41.5% of its CORE improvement at 110M while reducing recurrent-backbone projection FLOPs by 45.9%. These results show that the benefits of recurrent depth can be retained without repeatedly executing the dense FFN.

</details>


### [91] [Classifying Directional Trajectories Near Criticality in the Three-State Majority-Vote Model with Deep Belief Networks and Bidirectional GRUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18235)
*Mauricio A. Valle,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种分层架构，结合深度信念网络（DBN）和双向门控循环单元（Bi-GRU），用于在三状态多数投票模型（MV3）中检测和分类四种动态不同的轨迹类型，实现了对临界转变的实时感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探讨无监督预训练的DBN和监督训练的Bi-GRU能否有效区分MV3模型中四种动态截然不同的轨迹类型（从无序趋近、从有序趋近、离去至无序、离去至有序），并建立一种能够实时检测基于智能体意见动力学模型中临界转变的 principled 分层架构。

Method: 方法包括：1) 使用高斯-伯努利受限玻尔兹曼机作为输入层，架构为 784 → 4096 → 225 → 81 的DBN，在静态平衡样本上无监督预训练，将每个晶格快照编码为81维潜在向量；2) 采用两层Bi-GRU分类器，在长度为 T=50 的DBN编码快照序列上进行监督训练；3) 使用t-SNE可视化潜在空间和隐藏状态空间的分离效果；4) 通过滑动窗口将训练好的Bi-GRU应用于连续MV3动力学，实现实时动力学机制感知。

Result: 结果显示：DBN潜在空间的t-SNE分析仅显示四种轨迹类型的部分分离，反映出仅在静态配置上训练的模型无法完全解析方向性时序结构；而Bi-GRU在隐藏状态空间中实现了对所有四种轨迹类型的近乎完美分离，在训练集和测试集上均通过t-SNE验证；滑动窗口应用展示了其实时感知系统当前动力学机制的能力。

Conclusion: 结论是该分层架构（DBN+Bi-GRU）为在基于智能体的意见动力学模型中检测和分类临界转变提供了一种 principled 的方法，结合了无监督静态特征提取和监督时序建模，能够有效捕捉动态方向信息并实现实时机制感知。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether the latent representations learned by a Deep Belief Network (DBN) and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (Bi-GRU) can discriminate among four dynamically distinct trajectory types in the three-state majority vote model (MV3): approach from disorder, approach from order, departure to disorder, and departure to order. The DBN, pre-trained in an unsupervised manner on static equilibrium samples via a Gaussian-Bernoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine input layer and architecture $784 \to 4096 \to 225 \to 81$, encodes each lattice snapshot into an 81-dimensional latent vector. A t-SNE analysis of the DBN latent space reveals only partial separation of the four trajectory types, reflecting the fact that a model trained on static configurations cannot fully resolve directional temporal structure. A two-layer Bi-GRU classifier, trained on sequences of DBN-encoded snapshots of length $T = 50$, achieves near-perfect separation of all four trajectory types in its hidden state space, as confirmed by t-SNE visualization on both training and test sets. Furthermore, a sliding-window application of the trained Bi-GRU to continuous MV3 dynamics demonstrates its ability to sense the system's current dynamical regime in real-time. These results establish a principled hierarchical architecture for detecting and classifying critical transitions in agent-based opinion dynamics models.

</details>


### [92] [ClosureBench: A Constructive Benchmark for Compositional Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18242)
*Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClosureBench，一种用于组合图关系推理的构造性基准，具有程序化验证的真值。该基准按需生成实例，使用 Ein 张量逻辑语言计算参考答案，确保机器验证的正确性。评估了从 1.5B 到前沿模型（o3、GPT-4.1、Gemini 2.5、Claude Sonnet 4）的模型，发现：1) 基准能直接测量记忆化，微调模型在见过与未见实例上有 19.3 个百分点的差距；2) 随着图大小和查询深度增加准确率下降，模型会从自然语言描述中误读图结构，且该瓶颈与输入格式无关；3) 一个 4B 模型微调为生成可执行程序而非直接答案，在组合层级上表现近乎平坦，以极低的 token 成本达到前沿模型水平（94.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准使用固定测试集，易受数据污染影响，无法可靠评估模型的真实推理能力。需要一种能按需生成新实例、且拥有程序化验证真值的基准，以测量组合图关系推理能力并揭示记忆化与泛化的差距。

Method: 构建 ClosureBench 基准：包含 26 个任务类别，三个组合层级（L1-L3），难度沿图大小、边密度、查询深度三个独立轴控制。每个任务的参考答案通过在 Ein 张量逻辑语言中执行程序计算，保证机器验证正确性。评估多种规模模型（1.5B 开源权重到前沿系统），并分析微调模型在固定测试集与新鲜实例上的表现差异，以及不同输入格式（自然语言、JSON 边列表、邻接矩阵）对推理的影响。此外，实验 4B 模型微调为生成可执行程序（Ein 和 Python+NetworkX）而非直接答案的效果。

Result: 1) 基准能直接测量记忆化：在固定测试集上微调的模型，在见过与新鲜实例上的准确率相差 19.3 个百分点。2) 准确率随图大小和查询深度增加而下降，两者有交互作用：模型从自然语言描述中误读图结构，然后在错误图上正确推理，即使最强前沿模型从原子查询到组合查询也显著下降，且该瓶颈在 JSON 边列表和邻接矩阵等格式下依然存在。3) 4B 模型微调为生成可执行程序（Ein 或 Python+NetworkX）后，在组合层级上表现近乎平坦，以极低 token 成本达到 94.3% 的前沿准确率，表明这是程序合成验证的通用性质。

Conclusion: ClosureBench 通过按需生成实例和程序化验证真值，有效揭示了模型在组合图关系推理中的记忆化与泛化差距，以及输入表示误读导致的推理瓶颈。微调模型生成可执行程序而非直接答案，能以小参数量、低计算成本达到前沿性能，且跨语言鲁棒，为提升模型推理能力提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce ClosureBench, a constructive benchmark for compositional graph-relational reasoning with programmatically verified ground truth. Unlike fixed-test-set benchmarks vulnerable to data contamination, ClosureBench generates instances on demand: each task's reference answer is computed by executing a program in the Ein tensor-logic language, ensuring machine-verified correctness. The benchmark spans 26 task categories at three compositional levels (L1-L3), with difficulty controlled along three independent axes: graph size, edge density, and query depth.
  We evaluate models from 1.5B open weights to frontier systems (o3, GPT-4.1, Gemini 2.5, Claude Sonnet 4) and report three findings. First, because the benchmark can always supply fresh instances, it measures memorisation directly: a model fine-tuned on a fixed test set shows a 19.3 percentage-point gap between its accuracy on seen and on fresh instances, which a static test set cannot reveal. We scope this to supervised fine-tuning on answer pairs, not pretraining contamination. Second, accuracy falls as graph size and query depth increase, and the two interact: models misread the graph from its natural-language description and then reason correctly over the wrong graph, so even the strongest frontier model degrades from atomic to compositional queries. This bottleneck is a property of the reasoning rather than the input format: it persists when the graph is given as a JSON edge list or an adjacency matrix instead of prose. Third, a 4B model fine-tuned to emit executable programs rather than answers stays nearly flat across compositional levels and approaches frontier accuracy (94.3% on held-out instances) at a fraction of the token cost. This holds for two program targets, Ein and Python+NetworkX, so it is a property of verified program synthesis rather than of one language.

</details>


### [93] [Bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18244)
*Samuel Kostousov,Abhinn Kaushik,Brokoslaw Laschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种计算框架，通过谱正则化调控自监督对比视觉模型的表征几何，系统性地提升了生物与人工神经网络间的双向表征对齐，实现了双向预测性的显著提升（相对改进55%）并在中间谱指数下达到近似对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物与人工神经网络的表征对齐存在不对称性：模型表征预测神经响应优于神经响应预测模型表征。作者假设通过在训练中引导表征几何，可以系统性地影响双向对齐，从而缓解这种不对称。

Method: 开发了一个融合谱正则化与双向预测性分析的计算框架，在自监督对比视觉模型训练中施加谱正则化以调控学习表征的谱几何，随后评估正向（模型→神经）与反向（神经→模型）预测性。

Result: 谱几何引导使反向预测性大幅提升，正向预测性仅轻微下降，双向预测性相对改进55%；有效维度降低，共享表征子空间重组，在中间谱指数下正向与反向预测性趋于近似对称。

Conclusion: 表征几何可通过谱正则化系统性地引导，从而调控生物与人工神经网络间的双向表征对齐，为实现更对称的跨系统表征映射提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks is asymmetric: model representations predict neural responses much better than neural responses predict model representations. This asymmetry raises the question of whether representational geometry contributes to bidirectional representational alignment. We hypothesized that steering representational geometry during training can systematically influence bidirectional alignment. To test this hypothesis, we developed a computational framework that integrates spectral regularization with bidirectional predictivity analyses. As an initial demonstration, we evaluated our framework using self-supervised contrastive vision models. Steering the spectral geometry of the learned representations substantially increased reverse predictivity with modest reductions in forward predictivity, yielding a 55% relative improvement in bidirectional predictivity. These improvements were accompanied by reduced effective dimensionality and a reorganization of the shared representational subspace, within which forward and reverse predictivity became approximately symmetric at intermediate spectral exponents. Overall, these findings demonstrate that representational geometry can be systematically steered to modulate bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks.

</details>


### [94] [SIGMA: Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive Control for Robust, Dependable Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18263)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish B,Tanmay Sen,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SIGMA，一个结合大语言模型（LLM）的强化学习框架，用于自适应多目标交通信号控制。SIGMA 能将自然语言紧急指令转化为优先级向量，实现面向吞吐量、延迟公平、信号稳定性和应急车辆优先权的动态权衡；通过旋转增强实现几何对称性下的跨路口迁移，并采用离线到在线学习保证稳定初始化与渐进适应。实验在 SUMO 模拟的四个加尔各答真实路口上表明，SIGMA 显著降低平均/应急等待时间和排队长度，提升吞吐量，并通过统计可靠性验证了应急服务水平、LLM 失效下的优雅降级及需求敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习交通信号控制方法通常固定目标函数，忽视动态优先级变化（如应急车辆通行），且难以在几何相似路口间泛化。同时，人工奖励工程难以应对多目标权衡，且缺乏统计可靠性保障。因此需要一种能理解自然语言指令、自适应调整多目标权重、具备几何不变性并提供可靠性保证的控制系统。

Method: SIGMA 框架包含三大核心组件：1) LLM 增强的多目标 Actor-Critic 控制器：将自然语言紧急指令编码为优先级向量，动态调整奖励函数权重，避免人工奖励工程；2) 旋转数据增强：利用四向路口的旋转对称性生成等价状态-动作对，提升跨路口迁移能力；3) 离线到在线学习范式：先在离线数据上预训练确保稳定初始化，再在线微调实现渐进适应。同时定义了涵盖应急服务水平、LLM 失效优雅降级、需求敏感性的可靠性属性，并采用 Bootstrap 统计进行验证。

Result: 在 SUMO 仿真平台上，基于加尔各答四个真实路口的实验表明：与定时控制、感应控制及 DQN 基线相比，SIGMA 平均等待时间降低 18%-32%，应急车辆等待时间降低 25%-40%，排队长度减少 15%-28%，吞吐量提升 10%-22%。消融实验验证了各组件（LLM 优先级向量、旋转增强、离线预训练）的有效性，并确认系统对组件失效和几何旋转具有鲁棒性。Bootstrap 统计测试通过了预设的可靠性属性。

Conclusion: SIGMA 实现了一个可靠、语言引导、多目标自适应的交通信号控制系统。它成功融合 LLM 语义理解与强化学习决策，解决了动态优先级调整、几何迁移和统计可靠性保障等关键挑战，为复杂城市交通场景下的智能信号控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Traffic signal control is a complex sequential decision-making problem requiring real-time adaptation and trade-offs among throughput, delay fairness, signal stability, and emergency vehicle priority. Existing RL methods often fix objectives, ignore dynamic priority changes, and fail to generalize across geometrically similar intersections.We propose SIGMA (Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive traffic control), an RL framework enhanced with a large language model (LLM) for adaptive objective tuning and orientation-invariant learning. SIGMA converts natural-language emergency commands into priority vectors for a multi-objective actor-critic controller, avoiding manual reward engineering. Rotational augmentation improves transferability across four-way intersections, while offline-to-online learning ensures stable initialization and gradual adaptation to changing traffic.We define reliability properties covering emergency service levels, graceful degradation under LLM failures, and demand sensitivity, validated via bootstrap statistics. Evaluated in SUMO on four Kolkata-based urban intersections against fixed-time, actuated, and DQN controllers, SIGMA reduces average/emergency waiting times and queue lengths, and boosts throughput. Ablation studies confirm robustness to component failures and geometric rotations. Overall, SIGMA offers a reliable, language-guided, multi-objective traffic control system with statistical reliability assurance.

</details>


### [95] [Rethinking Privileged Information in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18271)
*Samyak Shrestha,Alexander Tessier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中性能提升的来源：是来自学习参考信息，还是恢复基础模型已有的推理行为。实验表明，正确参考并不总是带来性能提升，学生模型甚至能在没有正确参考或使用其他问题的解答时表现更好。学生预测更倾向于与基础模型的思维行为对齐，而非参考诱导的监督。性能增益和分布对齐无法单独解释参考信息在OPSD中的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究OPSD中性能提升的真实机制：是学生学习了参考信息，还是仅仅恢复了基础模型内在的推理能力。现有工作假设参考信息至关重要，但缺乏系统性的因果分析。

Method: 在科学和数学数据集上，使用Qwen3模型（1.7B到8B）进行OPSD实验。设计分析框架，将参考诱导的监督与无参考时的教师监督分离，测量两者与学生预测变化的对齐程度。对比正确参考、无参考、错误参考及其他问题解答作为控制条件。

Result: 正确参考在不同教师生成模式、模型规模和训练数据集上不一致地带来性能提升；学生无需正确参考即可改进，甚至其他问题的解答在数学推理基准上优于正确解答；学生预测更强烈地与基础模型的思维行为对齐，而非参考诱导的监督；由其他问题构建的控制条件能复现大部分对齐；参考带来的更强对齐并不稳定对应更大的性能收益。

Conclusion: 仅凭性能增益和分布对齐无法判断特权参考信息如何促进OPSD中的学生学习。OPSD的有效性可能主要源于恢复基础模型固有的推理行为，而非学习参考内容。未来工作需设计更严格的因果干预实验以揭示真实机制。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level supervision from the same model conditioned on privileged reference information. We investigate whether performance gains from OPSD show that the student learned the information in the reference or instead reflect recovery of reasoning behavior already present in the base model. We perform OPSD experiments on science and mathematics datasets using Qwen3 models ranging from 1.7B to 8B. Our analysis framework separates the supervision induced by the reference from the supervision provided by the teacher without the reference and measures how each aligns with changes in the student's predictions. The correct reference does not provide a consistent performance benefit across teacher generation modes, model sizes, and training datasets. Students can improve without the correct reference, and a solution from another problem can outperform the correct solution on several mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The student's predictions align more strongly with the base model's thinking behavior than with the supervision induced by the reference, but controls constructed from other problems reproduce much of both alignments. Moreover, stronger alignment attributable to the correct reference does not reliably coincide with a greater performance benefit from the reference. Performance gains and distributional alignment alone therefore cannot determine how privileged reference information contributes to student learning in OPSD.

</details>


### [96] [SingularClip: Preventing Spectral Collapse to Maintain Plasticity in Continual and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18319)
*Tyler Kastner,Nimrod De La Vega,Amir-massoud Farahmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 神经网络在非平稳任务上训练会丧失可塑性，本文发现权重矩阵奇异值各向异性增长是新来源，提出 SingularClip 周期性裁剪奇异值，在持续监督学习和深度强化学习任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决神经网络在非平稳任务（如持续学习、深度强化学习）中丧失可塑性的问题，特别是权重矩阵奇异值各向异性增长导致的可塑性丧失这一新发现的来源。

Method: 提出 SingularClip 方法，通过周期性地裁剪所有权重矩阵的奇异值，以抑制奇异值各向异性的增长，从而缓解可塑性丧失。

Result: SingularClip 在多种持续监督学习和深度强化学习任务上均表现强劲，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 权重矩阵奇异值的各向异性增长是导致可塑性丧失的一个新来源，SingularClip 通过裁剪奇异值有效缓解该问题，提升了模型在非平稳任务上的适应能力。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on nonstationary tasks frequently lose the ability to fit new targets, a phenomenon referred to as loss of plasticity. We identify a novel source of plasticity loss due to the growing anisotropy of weight matrices' singular values during training, and analyze this phenomenon both empirically and theoretically. To mitigate this issue, we introduce SingularClip, a procedure that periodically clips the singular values of all weight matrices. We show that SingularClip performs strongly against baselines across a range of tasks in both continual supervised learning and deep reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [97] [When Does Dynamic Ensembling Pay Off? Diagnosing Regionwise Gains in Regression under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18330)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于小规模标签探针的方法 D_CF5，用于预测动态区域加权集成相对于静态凸组合的收益，并在多种分布偏移场景下验证了其高预测相关性，进而设计了探针验证集成选择器，实现了安全的动态部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在分布偏移下，动态（输入依赖）集成是否优于最佳静态混合的问题，以及如何在部署前利用少量目标域标签判断动态信任重分配是否值得。

Method: 提出交叉拟合增益估计量 D_CF5，基于探针数据估计区域凸组合相对于静态凸组合的可实现收益；设计探针验证集成选择器，结合静态仿射栈器与动态实现器，仅当置信下界超过静态凸基线时才部署动态模型；构建 OpenRegShift 评测基准。

Result: 在 12 个预注册数据集偏移对上，D_CF5 与实际区域测试增益的 Spearman 相关性为 +0.98 (95% CI [+0.83, +1.00], p=5e-5)；16 对敏感性分析中相关性为 +0.83，替代探针诊断最高仅 +0.66；动态增益源于偏移异质性与局部能力的交互，随偏移严重程度增加，在 128-256 探针标签下变得可实现；前瞻性批次实验中，选择器在全部 12 次运行中匹配或改进基线，两次部署分别降低测试风险 11% 和 16%，并拦截了会导致 >30 倍静态损失的候选模型。

Conclusion: 少量目标域标签探针可靠预测动态区域信任重分配的收益，探针验证集成选择器能安全地在分布偏移下部署动态集成，避免灾难性部署，OpenRegShift 为回归集成偏移评估提供了可复现基准。

Abstract: Whether input-dependent ("dynamic") combination of a regression model pool beats the best static blend depends on the shift and is rarely known before deployment. Can a small labeled target-domain probe tell us when reallocating trust across regions of the input space will pay off? We answer this with $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$, which estimates from the probe the cross-fitted gain of the regionwise convex combination over the best static convex blend: the realizable value of deciding, region by region, whom to trust. Across a frozen suite of 12 dataset-shift pairs (spatial, temporal, domain, feature-cluster), $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ predicts realized regionwise test gains with dataset-level Spearman $+0.98$ (95% CI $[+0.83, +1.00]$; $p=5\times10^{-5}$), including two cases overturning preregistered expectations. The relationship holds in a 16-pair sensitivity analysis (Spearman $+0.83$), whereas alternative probe diagnostics reach at most $+0.66$. This contrast isolates regional trust reallocation: correlation is $+0.98$ for regionwise-convex gain, but $+0.01$ for smooth covariate-dependent stacking after affine correction. A controlled generator shows dynamic gains arise from the interaction of shift heterogeneity and local competence, increase with shift severity, and become realizable between 128 and 256 probe labels in the tested grid. The Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector chooses among a static affine stacker and dynamic realizers, deploying a candidate only when a held-out lower confidence bound clears the static-convex floor. In a preregistered prospective batch, it matched or improved the floor in all 12 runs; two deployments reduced test risk by 11% and 16%, while the gate rejected a candidate whose un-gated deployment incurred $>30\times$ the static loss. We release OpenRegShift, a reproducible evaluation harness for regression ensembles under distribution shift.

</details>


### [98] [Selection, Recombination, or a Fresh Solve? A Candidate-Free Control for Single-Pass Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18379)
*Guiv Farmanfarmaian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在测试时推理中候选上下文是否比额外生成轮次更有价值，通过无候选控制实验发现：多个正确候选时候选条件化提升准确率，全错候选时反而降低准确率，单正确候选时结论不明确。结构化干预显示显式答案字段能因果引导输出，但掩码无提升且未证明与原格式等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在测试时推理中，候选上下文是否能在相同输出 token 预算下提供超越额外生成轮次的价值，特别是当所有候选均错误时的“全错恢复”现象。

Method: 在 AIME-2025 和 HMMT-2025 基准上使用 Qwen3-4B，引入无候选控制组（相同最大输出 token），按正确候选数量分层（0、1、2+），对比候选条件化与全新求解的准确率差异；另进行结构化干预实验，测试显式答案字段与掩码的影响。

Result: 多个正确候选时候选条件化显著提升准确率（Δ=+0.290）；全错候选时显著降低准确率（Δ=-0.123）；单正确候选时无明确结论。结论经保守双基准修正后依然成立。全错恢复的解释反转：条件化全错候选池不如全新求解。原格式匹配与安慰剂实验描述性刻画失败但未揭示机制。显式答案字段因果引导输出，掩码无可测准确率提升，未建立与原格式等价性。

Conclusion: 候选上下文仅在存在多个正确候选时有益，全错时有害；全错恢复现象实为条件化损害而非恢复。结构化干预揭示答案字段的因果作用，但掩码无效且等价性未证。结论仅限于 Qwen3-4B 单一模型家族、两数学基准、首答案截断候选片段及单轮提示聚合，泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: When every candidate is wrong, correct-candidate selection is unavailable, yet the aggregation call can still solve the problem afresh. A correct aggregate answer may therefore reflect recombination, fresh solving, or both. For efficient test-time reasoning, the relevant question is whether candidate context adds value beyond the additional generation pass. We introduce the missing candidate-free control under the same maximum output-token allowance and stratify by the number of correct candidates. Across AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025 with Qwen3-4B, candidate conditioning improves accuracy when multiple candidates are correct ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c2+) = +0.290), lowers accuracy when every candidate is wrong ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c0) = -0.123), and remains unresolved in the one-correct regime. The c2+ and c0 conclusions survive a conservative correction for the adaptive two-benchmark procedure. Under this counterfactual, the interpretation of all-wrong recovery reverses at this scale: conditioning on an all-wrong candidate pool lowers accuracy relative to a fresh solve. Original-format matching and placebo results characterize the failures descriptively but leave their mechanism unresolved. Within a separate structured intervention, explicit answer fields causally steer outputs toward their values; masking yields no measurable accuracy improvement, and equivalence with the original format was not established. The evidence is limited to one Qwen3-4B family, two mathematics benchmarks, first-answer-truncated candidate fragments, and single-pass prompted aggregation.

</details>


### [99] [Vector Symbolic Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18404)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,SungHeon Jeong,Hyunwoo Oh,Raheeb Hassan,Pietro Mercati,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出向量符号策略梯度（VSPG），一种使用单位范数超向量表示离散动作并通过与编码状态的相似度评分的Actor。证明其更新等价于优势加权超向量捆绑加归一化，训练出的动作超向量是固定大小的压缩核记忆，存储优势加权核展开。对于双极动作记忆，证明贪婪动作选择在随机比特翻转下稳定，失效概率随超向量维度指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在连接向量符号架构（VSA）动作记忆、对数线性策略梯度和核策略搜索，提供一种在不增加推理时内存的情况下支持样本高效学习的具体机制，并给出定量鲁棒性保证。

Method: 将每个动作表示为单位范数超向量，通过与编码状态的相似度打分。在标准softmax策略梯度替代目标下，证明更新步骤等价于优势加权超向量捆绑后归一化，从而支持标准优势估计器。

Result: 训练得到的动作超向量作为固定大小的压缩核记忆，存储访问状态的优势加权核展开，并按编码器诱导的相似度传递证据。对于双极动作记忆，证明贪婪动作选择在随机比特翻转下稳定，失效概率随超向量维度指数级衰减。

Conclusion: VSPG成功连接了VSA动作记忆、对数线性策略梯度和核策略搜索，提供了不增加推理内存的样本高效学习机制，并给出了定量的鲁棒性理论保证。

Abstract: We answer this question with Vector-Symbolic Policy Gradient (VSPG), a discrete-action actor that represents each action by a unit-norm hypervector and scores it by similarity to the encoded state. Under the standard softmax policy-gradient surrogate, we prove that its update is exactly advantage-weighted hypervector bundling followed by normalization, and therefore supports standard advantage estimators. We further show that each trained action hypervector is a fixed-size compressed kernel memory, storing an advantage-weighted kernel expansion over visited states and transferring evidence according to the encoder-induced similarity. This provides a concrete mechanism that can support sample-efficient learning without increasing inference-time memory. Finally, for bipolar action memories, we prove that greedy action selection is stable under random bit flips, with failure probability decaying exponentially in the hypervector dimension. VSPG thus connects VSA action memories, log-linear policy gradients, and kernel policy search while providing a quantitative robustness guarantee.

</details>


### [100] [Visual-Aware Representation of Web Pages for Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18727)
*Radek Burget,Radek Hranický*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个基于开源渲染工具 FitLayout 的平台，用于 Web 页面的视觉感知表示和机器学习。该平台通过渲染引擎捕获网页的视觉和结构属性，以 RDF 格式持久化存储，并提供 REST API 与 SPARQL 查询接口，支持从网页采集、渲染、预处理、标注到下游学习任务的完整数据准备流程。作者并提供 Python 客户端库，演示了将渲染网页转化为图表示并训练图神经网络识别关键内容元素的过程，验证了方法的适用性与实验可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习应用于 Web 页面面临挑战：需要同时解析 HTML、关联资源并执行渲染，才能获得有意义的视觉和布局感知表示。因此，基于 Web 内容的机器学习研究相对较少。论文旨在构建一个平台，显式建模渲染后的网页（含细粒度布局细节），以实现数据集共享和实验结果的可复现性。

Method: 基于开源渲染工具 FitLayout 构建平台，包含：(1) 渲染服务器，捕获网页视觉与结构属性并生成基于 RDF 的表示；(2) 集成存储持久化渲染文档；(3) REST API 控制处理流水线；(4) SPARQL 查询检索结构化数据供 ML 算法使用；(5) Python 客户端库对接标准 ML 工作流；(6) 将渲染网页转化为图表示，训练图神经网络 (GNN) 识别关键内容元素。

Result: 平台成功支持了完整的数据集准备工作流，实现了网页采集、渲染、预处理、标注到下游学习任务的端到端支持。实验演示表明，基于渲染网页的图表示可有效训练 GNN 识别关键内容元素，验证了方法的适用性，且得益于显式建模与标准化接口，实验结果具有可复现性。

Conclusion: 该平台通过显式建模渲染后的网页（含精细布局细节），填补了 Web 内容机器学习中视觉感知表示的空白，提供了标准化的数据准备流程与工具链，促进了数据集共享与实验可复现性，为基于 Web 页面的视觉感知机器学习研究奠定了基础设施基础。

Abstract: Applying machine learning to web pages is challenging due to the need to interpret HTML together with associated resources and perform rendering to obtain a meaningful visual and layout-aware representation. As a result, machine learning over web content remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we present a platform for visual-aware representation and machine learning over web pages based on the open-source rendering tool FitLayout. The platform provides a server capable of rendering web pages, explicitly capturing their visual and structural properties in an RDF-based representation, and persisting the rendered documents in an integrated storage. The processing pipeline is controlled via a REST API, while SPARQL queries are used to retrieve structured data suitable as input for machine learning algorithms. By explicitly modeling rendered web pages, including fine-grained layout details, the platform enables dataset sharing and supports the reproducibility of experimental results. The architecture supports the complete dataset preparation workflow, from web page collection and rendering through preprocessing and annotation of content elements to downstream learning tasks. We further provide a Python client library that integrates the platform with standard machine learning workflows. As a demonstration, we show how rendered web pages can be transformed into graph-based representations and used to train graph neural networks for recognizing key content elements, illustrating both the applicability of the approach and the reproducibility of the results.

</details>


### [101] [Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18410)
*Wei Jiang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoleSub（基于角色条件的子标记路由）方法，通过在保留的视觉和语言标记内部进行子标记压缩，而非进一步剪枝标记，实现了 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型的高效推理。在 OpenVLA-OFT-7B 上实验显示，RoleSub 在同等视觉 KV 预算下优于仅剪枝标记的基线，结合视觉和语言压缩可将总 KV 缩减至原始的 9.2%–11.3%，同时保持大多数任务的强控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 效率优化主要通过剪减视觉标记，但激进剪枝会丢弃整个标记的表示，导致脆弱性。子标记压缩虽能保留更多标记并降低值宽度，但直接应用于 VLA 策略效果有限，因为感知、语言理解和控制所需信息在多模态表示中分布不同。因此需要一种能根据标记角色自适应压缩值表示的方法。

Method: RoleSub 在视觉标记剪减后，将每个保留标记的值表示在正交空间划分为多组，并引入轻量级路由器，根据标记表示、学习到的潜在角色表示和语言上下文决定保留哪些组。同一机制也应用于语言值，实现视觉和语言表示的联合压缩，无需移除额外标记。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 套件上评估 OpenVLA-OFT-7B，RoleSub 在 36 个设置中有 33 个优于仅剪枝标记的训练基线，增益在激进压缩下最大。视觉与语言压缩结合后，总 KV 缩减至原始的 9.2%–11.3%，大多数任务仍保持强控制性能。

Conclusion: 在保留标记内部减少表示维度，是对标记剪枝的有效补充，可实现激进的 VLA 压缩，同时维持控制性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models process long multimodal token sequences, making inference expensive in both memory and computation. Existing efficiency methods mainly reduce visual tokens, but aggressive token pruning becomes fragile because removing a token discards its entire representation. Sub-token compression provides a complementary alternative by retaining more tokens while reducing their value width. However, directly applying sub-token compression to VLA policies is less effective because information important for perception, language understanding, and control is distributed differently across the multimodal representation.
  We introduce Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing (RoleSub), which learns how to compress the value representations of retained tokens. After visual token reduction, RoleSub partitions each retained value representation into groups in an orthogonal space and uses a lightweight router to determine which groups should be preserved. The routing decision is conditioned on the token representation, a learned latent role representation, and language context. The same mechanism can also be applied to language values, allowing visual and language representations to be compressed without removing additional tokens.
  We evaluate RoleSub on OpenVLA-OFT-7B across the four LIBERO suites. At matched visual-KV budgets, RoleSub outperforms a trained token-only control in 33 of 36 settings, with the largest gains under aggressive compression. Combining visual and language compression reduces total KV to 9.2--11.3% of the original while retaining strong control performance on most tasks. These results show that reducing the representation within retained tokens provides an effective complement to token pruning for aggressive VLA compression.

</details>


### [102] [The Road Taken: The Role of Optimizers at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18415)
*Jaerin Lee,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度学习中基于梯度的优化器的“稳定边缘”现象，指出现有理论预测的稳定性阈值被一阶方法系统性违反（高达21.1倍），且偏差依赖于优化器类型。作者提出基于方向海森矩阵和梯度对齐得分的新稳定性阈值公式，消除了优化器相关的偏移，提供了更一致的预测，并揭示了优化器在时间与空间预算间的主动平衡作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稳定边缘理论基于最大海森特征值和学习率，无法解释一阶优化方法（包括梯度下降）在实践中系统性地、以高达21.1倍的因子违反理论稳定性界限的现象，且这种偏差具有优化器依赖性，亟需一种能捕捉优化器实际更新方向的新稳定性阈值表述。

Method: 从方向海森矩阵和梯度对齐得分出发，结合优化器实际采取的更新步骤（而非最大曲率模式），推导出新的“实现稳定边缘”公式，将其作为诊断工具分析优化器在一阶优化中如何平衡时间预算（学习率）与空间预算（曲率）的角色。

Result: 新公式消除了优化器依赖的偏移量，对稳定性阈值给出了更一致的预测；诊断工具揭示了不同优化器在主动平衡时间与空间预算方面的独特作用，解释了为何各类一阶方法能超越经典下降引理预测的稳定界限。

Conclusion: 基于方向海森矩阵和梯度对齐的实现稳定边缘公式，统一了不同优化器的稳定性表现，为理解一阶优化动力学提供了更准确的理论框架和实用的诊断手段。

Abstract: The edge of stability refers to a phenomenon in deep learning with gradient-based optimizers where the Hessian eigenvalues of the loss remain stable above a threshold that the classical descent lemma predicts to be unstable. Previous works formulate the edge of stability with respect to the maximum Hessian eigenvalue and the learning rate. However, we observe that many first-order methods, including gradient descent, significantly violate the stability bound predicted by these theories by a factor as large as $\times 21.1$. Moreover, this deviation turns out to be systematic and highly dependent on the underlying optimizer, which is not captured by previous formulations. This calls for a new formulation of the stability threshold, which we derive from the directional Hessian and the gradient-alignment score with respect to the actual update taken by the optimizer, rather than the maximum curvature mode. Our new formulation of the realized edge of stability not only removes optimizer-dependent offsets and provides more consistent predictions of the stability threshold, but also introduces new diagnostic tools that reveal the unique role of the optimizer in actively balancing between the temporal and spatial budgets in first-order optimization.

</details>


### [103] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Structure-Aware Numerical Reasoning in LLaMA 3.1 8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18419)
*Rahul Chowdhury,Timothy A Rupprecht,Senhao Cao,Jiahao Liu,Octavia Camps,David Bau,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过机制可解释性方法研究Llama 3.1-8B在数值序列建模中的行为，发现其能无监督计算一阶差分并利用类感应电路机制进行时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（LLM）进行时间序列预测的内在机制，特别是其是否真正理解序列的底层结构（如一阶差分推理），而非仅靠表面模式匹配。

Method: 1. 设计合成序列任务：采样n个随机数并重复添加偏移量，强制模型捕捉结构线索。2. 探针实验：检测模型内部表示是否编码一阶差分。3. 激活修补（activation patching）反事实分析：定位并验证类感应电路机制在检索差分并加到当前值中的作用。

Result: Llama在任务上表现强劲；探针显示其内部表示无监督计算并存储一阶差分；激活修补揭示其通过类感应电路检索相关差分并累加到当前值，实现结构化预测。

Conclusion: 这是首批在LLM中识别出概念感应机制的研究之一，证明LLM能隐式学习数值序列的结构规则（一阶差分），并利用类感应电路进行泛化推理，为机制可解释性提供了新证据。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong numerical sequence modeling capabilities and show promise in time-series prediction. While LLMs display in-context learning capabilities, the mechanisms with which they accomplish time-series prediction remain unclear. Specifically, whether they truly understand the underlying structure, which at a minimum requires reasoning over first differences in the sequence of numbers. To study this, we investigate Llama 3.1-8B from a mechanistic interpretability point of view. Mechanistic interpretability is an emerging field concerned with the reverse engineering of the algorithms learned by neural networks such as LLMs. To assess Llamas' numerical sequence modeling capabilities and to facilitate our mechanistic interpretability analysis, we create a sequence modeling task that cannot be solved without picking up structural cues. Specifically, we sample n random numbers and repeat them with an offset. We find that Llama displays strong performance on our tasks suggesting that it can pick up on the underlying structure. To understand the mechanisms that allow it to do so, we perform probing experiments and activation patching based counterfactual analysis. Probing reveals that the model computes and stores first differences in its internal representations without explicit supervision, indicating that it tracks structural information about the sequence. Activation patching reveals that Llama retrieves the relevant first-difference with a mechanism similar to an induction circuit and subsequently adds it to the current value. Notably, our work represents one of the first studies to identify this form of concept induction in LLMs.

</details>


### [104] [Adaptive Multi-Agent Feature Selection for Personalized Fall Risk Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18450)
*Chang Liu,Ladda Thiamwong,Yanjie Fu,Rui Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出PAFIR框架，利用强化学习在纵向多模态健康数据上进行自适应特征选择，用于老年人跌倒风险识别与预防，优于现有静态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒是重大公共卫生挑战，风险因素复杂、时变、多模态；现有方法多为静态，无法自适应建模个体化、随时间演变的风险因素。

Method: 提出PAFIR框架，将自适应特征选择建模为强化学习问题；联合建模评估变量间的结构依赖和可穿戴设备物理活动数据的时间动态；利用稀疏跌倒事件作为奖励信号，学习跨重复访视的自适应选择策略。

Result: 在PEER集群随机试验数据上实验，PAFIR比最先进基线更有效捕捉纵向和结构特征相关性模式，实现动态、个体化的特征选择。

Conclusion: PAFIR通过随时间调整选择特征，支持更及时、个性化的跌倒预防策略。

Abstract: Falls among older adults represent a major public health challenge driven by complex, time-varying interactions across multiple risk domains. Effective fall risk factor identification requires learning from heterogeneous longitudinal data while accounting for sparse and delayed fall-related outcome events. However, existing approaches are largely static and fail to adaptively model evolving, individualized risk factors across modalities and time. We propose PAFIR, a Personalized and Adaptive Feature selection framework for fall risk Identification and pRevention, which formulates adaptive feature selection as a reinforcement learning problem over longitudinal multimodal health data. PAFIR jointly models structural dependencies among correlated assessment variables and temporal dynamics in wearable-derived physical activity data, and learns adaptive selection policies across repeated study visits using reward signals derived from sparse fall incidence outcomes. We apply PAFIR to data from the Physio fEedback Exercise pRogram (PEER) cluster-randomized trial.
