Experimental results demonstrate that PAFIR more effectively captures longitudinal and structural patterns of feature relevance than state-of-the-art baselines, and enables dynamic, subject-specific feature selection. By adapting selected features over time, PAFIR supports more timely and personalized fall prevention strategies.

</details>


### [105] [Atrial Fibrillation Detection with Arbitrary Leads via a Codebook-Based Reconstruction-Classification Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18451)
*Hongtao Li,Jia Wei,Guoyao Li,Yuchen Lei,Guangnian Ma,Jia Xiao,Yuanjun Lai,Shuzhen Lv,Xueqiang Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为DCGCNet的新型端到端向量量化变分自编码器，用于稳健且可泛化的心房颤动（AF）检测，结合了局部-全局对比模块和自适应码本向量量化器，在多种数据集和噪声条件下均达到AUC>0.98的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实临床环境中，由于导联配置多变、跨数据集域偏移以及普遍存在的生理和技术伪影，可靠的心房颤动检测仍具挑战性，亟需一种鲁棒且泛化能力强的深度学习模型。

Method: 提出双码本图协作网络（DCGCNet），包含两大核心组件：1) 局部-全局对比模块，学习噪声不变表示；2) 自适应码本向量量化器，动态细化码本原型以更好地匹配输入数据分布，防止码本坍塌并增强泛化能力。模型联合执行AF分类和ECG重建任务。

Result: 在标准数据集内12导联评估中达到最先进性能；在七种不同的跨数据集泛化设置中均保持AUC>0.98；在基线漂移、工频干扰和肌电伪影等真实噪声条件下仍能维持高诊断准确率。

Conclusion: DCGCNet为鲁棒、可泛化且抗噪的AF检测建立了新基准，展现出在真实临床环境中部署的强大潜力。

Abstract: \textbf{Background and Objective}: Reliable atrial fibrillation (AF) detection from electrocardiogram (ECG) signals remains challenging in real-world clinical settings due to variable lead configurations, cross-dataset domain shifts, and pervasive physiological and technical artifacts. So we develop a robust and generalizable deep learning model for accurate AF detection.\\ \textbf{Methods}: We propose the Dual-Codebook Graph Collaborative Network (DCGCNet), a novel end-to-end vector-quantized variational autoencoder that jointly performs AF classification and ECG reconstruction. DCGCNet introduces two key components: (1) a Local-Global Contrastive Module for learning noise-invariant representations, and (2) an Adaptive Codebook Vector Quantizer that dynamically refines codebook prototypes to better align with input data distributions, thereby preventing codebook collapse and enhancing generalization.\\ \textbf{Results}: DCGCNet achieves state-of-the-art performance in standard intra-dataset 12-lead evaluation and demonstrates exceptional cross-dataset generalization across seven diverse settings, consistently attaining AUC > 0.98 in all cases. Furthermore, it maintains high diagnostic accuracy under realistic noisy conditions, including baseline wander, powerline interference, and EMG artifacts.\\ \textbf{Conclusions}: DCGCNet establishes a new benchmark for robust, generalizable, and noise-resilient AF detection, showing strong potential for deployment in real-world clinical environments.

</details>


### [106] [ERASE: EaRly bAckpropagation SchEdule for Faster Training of Modern Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18469)
*Ergan Shang,Flavio Sales Truzzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ERASE方法，利用Forward-Forward（FF）的detachment机制作为调度原语，将反向传播提前到独立CUDA流中执行，与后续前向计算重叠，提升轻量级模型训练吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级代理模型的小kernel导致现代加速器利用率低；传统训练将前向和后向作为不相交阶段调度，无法利用闲置算力。

Method: 重新诠释FF的detachment机制：分离块的输出移除下游梯度依赖，使其反向传播在前向结束后即可就绪。ERASE在独立CUDA流上启动每个分离子图的反向传播，与后续前向工作重叠执行。

Result: 轻量级Transformer执行轨迹验证了重叠效果及其极限（饱和设备的kernel无并发余量）；大规模CTR模型中分离六个稠密子架构，训练吞吐提升高达9.51%，归一化熵接近基线。

Conclusion: ERASE通过FF detachment实现前向后向流水线并行，有效提升轻量级模型训练硬件利用率与吞吐，且不损害模型质量。

Abstract: Lightweight proxy models enable rapid experimentation without repeatedly training frontier-scale systems, but their small kernels often leave modern accelerators underutilized. Conventional training compounds this inefficiency by scheduling the forward and backward passes as disjoint phases, so spare capacity in one cannot be filled by work from the other. We reinterpret the detachment mechanism of Forward-Forward (FF) as a scheduling primitive: given a local objective, detaching a block's output removes downstream gradient dependencies, making its backward pass ready when its forward pass finishes. ERASE launches each detached subgraph's backward pass early on a separate CUDA stream, overlapping it with subsequent forward work. Execution trace on a lightweight transformer demonstrates this overlap and its limit: a kernel that saturates the device leaves no capacity for concurrency. On a large-scale click-through-rate model, detaching six dense subarchitectures improves training throughput by up to $9.51\%$ while keeping normalized entropy close to the baseline.

</details>


### [107] [Physics-Unrolled Neural Operator for Wireless Field Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18495)
*Rafid Umayer Murshed,Saif Ur Rahman,Mingyue Tang,Elahe Soltanaghai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理展开混合神经算子(PU-HNO)，一种三级级联模型，用于从低保真射线追踪输出和场景先验预测高保真室内无线电图，通过逐步捕获反射、衍射和散射效应，在条件无偏标签噪声下证明模型能学习稳定传播结构并超越训练标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线电图对无线决策任务至关重要，但精细空间细节受复杂传播效应支配，高精度模拟成本高昂。机器学习可避免对每个场景运行昂贵仿真，但大规模生成高质量训练标签困难：廉价标签来自有限射线仿真，虽比低保真输入丰富，却残留蒙特卡洛噪声。

Method: PU-HNO采用三级级联架构：第一级捕获反射效应，第二级捕获衍射效应，第三级捕获散射效应，而非将无线电图视为通用图像处理。输入为低保真射线追踪输出和场景先验，逐步细化预测。理论上证明在条件无偏标签噪声下，模型能学习稳定传播结构并超越自身训练标签。

Result: 在多样化楼层平面实验中，PU-HNO在图像质量指标和无线部署指标上均优于图像到图像基线、无线学习模型和单体神经算子。

Conclusion: PU-HNO通过物理展开的混合神经算子级联，有效解决了有限射线仿真标签噪声问题，实现了从低保真到高保真无线电图的精准预测，为无线网络规划提供了高效且高精度的工具。

Abstract: Radio maps are essential for wireless decision-making tasks such as access-point placement, coverage planning, and localization, but their fine spatial details are governed by complex propagation effects and are costly to simulate accurately. Machine learning offers a path to high-fidelity radio-map prediction without running expensive high-fidelity simulations for every scene. However, generating high-quality training labels at scale is also difficult: the affordable labels come from finite-ray simulations, which are richer than low-fidelity inputs but carry residual Monte Carlo noise. We address this challenge with Physics-Unrolled Hybrid Neural Operator (PU-HNO), a three-stage cascade that predicts high-fidelity indoor radio maps from low-fidelity ray-tracing outputs and scene priors by progressively capturing reflection, diffraction, and scattering effects, rather than treating radio maps as generic images. We prove that, under conditionally unbiased label noise, the model can learn stable propagation structure and outperform its own training labels. Experiments across diverse floorplans show that PU-HNO outperforms image-to-image baselines, wireless learning models, and monolithic neural operators across both image-quality and wireless deployment metrics.

</details>


### [108] [Tianmu-TC: Physics-constraints Generative Artificial Intelligence for Global Tropical Cyclone Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18500)
*Shiqi Zhang,Pan Mu,Cheng Huang,Hanting Yan,Yuchao Zhu,Jinglin Zhang,Shengyong Chen,Shoujuan Shu,Cong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Tianmu-TC，一种基于物理约束的生成式AI框架，用于全球热带气旋（TC）预测，在显著降低计算成本的同时，优于现有的确定性和集合气象AI模型及权威数值天气预报（NWP）系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带气旋预测面临大气混沌特性和初始条件误差快速放大的挑战，导致预测不确定性增加；现有的数值天气预报和深度学习模型计算量大，且在复杂气象场景下常失效。

Method: Tianmu-TC是一个物理约束生成框架，利用西北太平洋数据训练，通过引入物理约束生成可控输出并降低不确定性，从而提高预测可靠性。

Result: 实验表明，Tianmu-TC在全球海洋盆地中优于确定性和集合气象AI模型及ECMWF等权威NWP系统，计算成本显著降低；在数据稀疏、异常路径、快速增强和减弱等挑战性场景中表现良好。

Conclusion: 基于物理约束的生成式AI为可靠、高效的全球热带气旋预测提供了有前景的新途径。

Abstract: Tropical cyclones (TCs) pose severe risks from strong winds and heavy rainfall. However, forecasting their track and intensity remains challenging due to chaotic atmosphere and the rapid amplification of initial condition errors, leading to growing forecast uncertainty. While numerical weather prediction (NWP) and deep learning models have made progress, they remain computationally demanding and often fail under complex meteorological scenarios. Here, we present Tianmu-TC, a physics-constraints generative framework for global TC forecasting. Trained on Western North Pacific data, Tianmu-TC leverages physics-constraints to generate controllable outputs with reduced uncertainty thus improving forecast reliability. Experiments show Tianmu-TC outperforms deterministic and ensemble meteorological artificial intelligence models and authoritative NWP systems such as ECMWF in global ocean basins, with significantly lower computational cost. We further show Tianmu-TC performs well in challenging scenarios such as data sparsity, anomaly tracks, rapid intensification and weakening. These findings suggest physics-constraints generative AI offers a promising approach for reliable, efficient global TC forecasting.

</details>


### [109] [LLM-Powered Predictive Decision-Making for Sustainable Data Center Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18503)
*Hanzhao Wang,Jingxuan Wu,Yumeng Li,Yu Pan,Guanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的预测性调度系统，用于数据中心GPU资源分配，通过预测执行时间、能耗等指标并结合实时调度算法，在实际数据中心合作中实现了能耗降低32%和等待时间减少30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型等AI工作负载激增，数据中心面临巨大的能源和资源消耗压力，亟需提升运营效率并降低环境影响，推动AI基础设施的可持续发展。

Method: 系统包含两个核心组件：1) 基于LLM的预测模型，从源代码预测执行时间、能耗等关键指标，可扩展至水资源使用、碳排放等可持续性指标；2) 实时调度算法，根据预测结果分配GPU资源，联合优化能耗和排队延迟。该方法具有推理快、泛化能力强、训练数据需求少的特点。

Result: 通过与数据中心合作验证，该框架实现了32%的能耗降低和30%的等待时间减少，展示了在实际生产环境中的显著效果。

Conclusion: 该LLM驱动的预测性调度框架为数据中心调度提供了实用解决方案，具有推进AI驱动基础设施可持续发展目标的强大潜力。

Abstract: The growing demand for AI-driven workloads, particularly from Large Language Models (LLMs), has raised concerns about the significant energy and resource consumption in data centers. This work introduces a novel LLM-based predictive scheduling system designed to enhance operational efficiency while reducing the environmental impact of data centers. Our system utilizes an LLM to predict key metrics such as execution time and energy consumption from source code, and it has the potential to extend to other sustainability-focused metrics like water usage for cooling and carbon emissions, provided the data center can track such data. The predictive model is followed by a real-time scheduling algorithm that allocates GPU resources, aiming to improve sustainability by optimizing both energy consumption and queuing delays. With fast inference times, the ability to generalize across diverse task types, and minimal data requirements for training, our approach offers a practical solution for data center scheduling. This framework demonstrates strong potential for advancing sustainability objectives in AI-driven infrastructure. Through our collaboration with a data center, we achieved a 32% reduction in energy consumption and a 30% decrease in waiting time.

</details>


### [110] [Evaluating and Explaining Prompt Sensitivity of LLMs Using Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18539)
*Ruiyang Qin,Qingzhuo Wang,Tian Wang,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于交互的提示敏感度（IPS）指标，通过分解LLM输出得分为交互项来细粒度分析提示敏感性。实验发现监督微调、模型规模增大、稠密架构和少样本学习四个因素均能降低提示敏感度，其共同机制是降低低阶交互（涉及少量输入变量的交互）的提示敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常通过比较LLM最终输出变化来评估提示敏感度，但这种粗粒度指标无法解释内部原因。LLM输出即使不变，内部交互结构也可能剧烈波动，需要细粒度工具揭示提示敏感性的内在机制。

Method: 将LLM输出得分分解为一组交互项，每个交互表示输入变量集合的非线性关系。提出交互式提示敏感度（IPS）指标，量化引入细微提示变化时交互项的变化量。在50个开源LLM上应用IPS指标进行实证分析。

Result: 发现四个降低提示敏感度的因素：监督微调、增加模型规模、稠密架构、少样本学习。更关键的是揭示共同机制：这四个因素均倾向于降低低阶交互（涉及少量输入变量的交互）的提示敏感度。即使LLM输出保持不变，细微提示变化也会导致交互项严重不稳定。

Conclusion: 基于交互的细粒度分析揭示了提示敏感性的内在机制。低阶交互的稳定性是降低LLM提示敏感度的关键，四种常用技术手段通过同一机制发挥作用，为提升LLM鲁棒性提供了理论指导和度量工具。

Abstract: The remarkable capabilities of large language models (LLMs) are often undermined by their instability. Even subtle and semantically irrelevant changes in prompts can cause dramatic fluctuations in performance, a phenomenon known as prompt sensitivity. Previous studies typically evaluate prompt sensitivity by comparing the LLM's final outputs when prompts change. However, such coarse-grained metrics fail to explain the internal reasons for prompt sensitivity. In this paper, we introduce interactions as a fine-grained tool to analyze prompt sensitivity of LLMs. Specifically, we decompose the output score of the LLM into a set of interactions. Each interaction represents a nonlinear relationship involving a set of input variables. We discover that subtle changes to prompts can trigger severe instability in interactions, even when the outputs of the LLM remain the same. To this end, we propose an Interaction-based Prompt Sensitivity (IPS) metric by quantifying changes in interactions when we introduce subtle changes to prompts. We apply the IPS metric to 50 open-source LLMs and uncover four factors that reduce the prompt sensitivity of LLMs, including supervised fine-tuning, increased model scales, dense architectures, and few-shot learning. More crucially, we discover a common mechanism by which these four factors reduce prompt sensitivity: all four factors tend to reduce the prompt sensitivity of low-order interactions (i.e., interactions involving few input variables).

</details>


### [111] [MARCUS: Missing-Aware Region Representation with Contextual Urban Signals for Rent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18546)
*Chenya Huang,Bin Liang,Zhidong Li,Yuxi Lu,Kunqi Li,Justin Wang,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCUS，一种缺失感知的区域表征学习模型，将多模态城市数据中的缺失模式视为语义信号而非噪声，通过三阶段学习（内部学习、交互学习、融合）提升租金预测性能，在悉尼和纽约数据集上分别将MAE降低51.35%和12.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态城市区域表征学习方法通常通过插补处理缺失数据，将缺失视为噪声而忽略其潜在语义价值；缺失模式本身可能蕴含城市功能、经济活动等上下文信息，直接利用缺失模式可增强表征能力。

Method: MARCUS包含三个阶段：1) Intra Learning（内部学习）：联合编码观测特征与缺失模式；2) Inter Learning（交互学习）：估计模态可靠性以指导跨模态交互；3) Fusion（融合）：采用缺失感知与时间感知门控机制生成最终区域嵌入。

Result: 在真实租金预测任务上，MARCUS达到最优性能：悉尼数据集MAE较最佳基线降低51.35%，纽约数据集降低12.62%；消融实验与额外随机缺失实验进一步验证方法有效性。

Conclusion: 将缺失模式建模为城市上下文信号而非简单插补，能显著提升多模态城市区域表征质量，为长期趋势与季节性波动的租金预测等任务提供更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal urban data has expanded the applications of urban region representation learning, such as functional zone identification and real estate appraisal, but also introduces challenges caused by data incompleteness. Existing studies usually handle missing data through imputation, treating missingness as noise while ignoring its potential semantic value. To address this issue, we propose MARCUS, a missing-aware region representation model that treats missingness as a contextual urban signal. MARCUS models missingness in three stages: Intra Learning jointly encodes observed features and missing patterns, Inter Learning estimates modality reliability to guide cross-modal interaction, and Fusion uses missing-aware and time-aware gating to generate the final region embedding. We apply MARCUS to rent prediction, a task with long-term trends and seasonal fluctuations, using real-world datasets from Sydney and New York. Experimental results show that MARCUS achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing MAE by 51.35% on Sydney and 12.62% on New York compared with the best baselines. Additional experiments, including an imputation-based ablation study and randomized additional-missingness analysis, further demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [112] [Performance Drift Detection in Machine Learning as a Service (MLaaS) for IoT Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18555)
*Deepak Kanneganti,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Mohammad Mostakim Fattah,Erik Elmroth,Aneesh Krishna,Monowar Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向IoT环境的MLaaS性能漂移检测框架(MPDD)，包含MLaaS提取模型和自适应时序漂移检测机制(APDDM)，实验表明MPDD比基线方法准确率提升22-25%，APDDM平均准确率增益约4%，漏检率降低约9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLaaS在IoT环境中广泛应用，但IoT数据分布动态变化及MLaaS周期性更新导致性能漂移；客户端作为黑盒用户无法访问内部数据和参数，使得漂移检测极具挑战性。

Method: 首先构建MLaaS提取模型，从输入输出对学习服务行为并识别预测影响特征；在此基础上提出MPDD模型，联合捕捉输入数据和MLaaS行为的变化；进一步设计APDDM机制，根据行为和数据变化动态调整监控频率，实现及时漂移检测。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，MPDD比基线漂移检测方法准确率提升22-25%；APDDM相比固定间隔监控，平均准确率增益约4%，漏检率降低约9%。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPDD框架结合自适应时序检测机制，能有效解决IoT环境下MLaaS性能漂移的黑盒检测问题，显著提高检测准确性和及时性，为服务管理提供有力支持。

Abstract: Machine Learning as a Service (MLaaS) is a powerful cloud paradigm enabling data-driven intelligent applications in Internet of Things (IoT) environments, widely adopted across healthcare, smart homes, and industry due to its cost-effectiveness. However, the dynamic nature of IoT frequently alters data distributions, affecting MLaaS stability, while periodic MLaaS updates further introduce performance drift. Unlike traditional ML systems, MLaaS clients operate as black-box users without access to internal data or parameters, making drift detection particularly challenging. To address this, we propose a novel MLaaS Performance Drift Detection framework for IoT environments. The framework first employs an MLaaS extraction model that learns service behavior from input-output pairs and identifies prediction-influenced features. Building on this, the proposed MLaaS Performance Drift Detection (MPDD) model jointly captures variations in input data and MLaaS behavior. We further design an Adaptive-Temporal Performance Drift Detection Mechanism (APDDM) that dynamically adjusts monitoring frequency based on behavioral and data variations, enabling timely drift detection for effective service management. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that MPDD achieves up to 22-25% accuracy improvement over baseline drift detection methods. APDDM provides an average accuracy gain of approximately 4% and reduces the miss detection rate by around 9% compared to fixed-interval monitoring.

</details>


### [113] [MorphoGP: A Nonparametric Framework for Predicting Equilibrium Beach Profiles Under Tidal Influence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18558)
*Xi Wu,Yanqing Wei,Hang Yin,Pengze Li,Hongshuai Qi,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MorphoGP框架，结合对比学习自动分类潮汐影响海滩形态、分类别高斯过程专家学习环境参数与剖面形状关联、门控网络概率加权集成预测，在180+中国海岸剖面数据上测试RMSE较基线降低59.3%至0.297米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验与数值模型在潮汐主导海滩系统中适应性受限，难以捕捉波浪-潮汐-沉积物高度非线性相互作用，亟需适应多样化海岸环境的数据驱动预测方法。

Method: 1) 基于对比学习的ContourCluster模型自动分类潮汐影响海滩形态；2) 每类形态内训练专用高斯过程专家，学习波浪、潮汐、沉积物等环境描述子与剖面形状的统计关联；3) 门控网络通过概率加权机制融合所有专家输出生成最终预测。

Result: 在中国海岸180余个潮汐影响海滩剖面数据评估中，MorphoGP测试RMSE较最佳基线降低约59.3%，最终RMSE达0.297米，显著优于传统经验模型及深度学习基线。

Conclusion: MorphoGP提供了一种融合物理先验的数据驱动工具，可用于潮汐影响下平衡海滩剖面预测与海岸管理；未来需进一步强化过程级物理机制耦合。

Abstract: The prediction of equilibrium beach profiles under tidal influence is of fundamental importance for sustainable coastal development, informing shoreline protection strategies and managing coastal ecosystems under changing environmental conditions. However, it remains challenging due to the highly nonlinear interactions among wave, tide, and sedimentary processes. Traditional empirical and numerical models often exhibit limited adaptability across diverse coastal environments, with especially pronounced limitations in beach systems where tidal processes are important . To improve data-driven prediction under these conditions, this study proposes MorphoGP, a unified category-specific Gaussian process framework for predicting equilibrium beach profiles (EBPs) under tidal influence. The framework first introduces a ContourCluster model based on contrastive learning to classify tide-influenced beach morphologies automatically. Within each morphological category, a specialized Gaussian process expert learns statistical associations between environmental descriptors including waves, tides, and sediments and the beach profile's shape. A Gating Net then integrates the outputs of all experts through a probabilistic weighting mechanism to produce the final prediction. Evaluated on data from over 180 beach profiles from tide-influenced coasts along the Chinese coast, MorphoGP achieves improved predictive performance compared with conventional and deep learning models, reducing the test RMSE by about 59.3\% compared with the best baseline and achieving a final RMSE of 0.297 m. The proposed framework provides a physically informed, data-driven tool for equilibrium beach-profile prediction under tidal influence and coastal management, while stronger process-level physical coupling remains an important direction for future development.

</details>


### [114] [NanoSleep: A Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Temporal Convolutional Network for Single-Channel Sleep Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18571)
*S M Asif Hossain,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NanoSleep，一种紧凑的混合时域卷积网络，用于单通道EEG自动睡眠分期分类，在保持高准确率的同时大幅降低模型规模，适用于可穿戴设备和资源受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型虽能实现高准确率的睡眠分期，但模型体积过大，难以部署在资源受限的可穿戴设备或家用监测设备上，因此需要一种兼顾精度与效率的轻量化模型。

Method: NanoSleep包含可学习的Sinc卷积前端、双分支特征提取器（融合多尺度时域和频域表示）、带通道重校准的门控扩张时域卷积主干网络、用于序列级解码的条件随机场（CRF），并采用加权校准焦点损失解决类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在Sleep-EDF和Sleep-EDF-Expanded数据集上采用受试者间交叉验证，NanoSleep持续优于六个代表性基线方法，消融实验验证了各主要组件的贡献。

Conclusion: NanoSleep在准确率与模型效率之间实现了有效平衡，适合部署于可穿戴设备、家庭睡眠监测及资源受限的临床应用中。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification from single-channel electroencephalography (EEG) is essential for wearable and home-based sleep monitoring. However, many deep learning models achieve high accuracy at the cost of large model sizes, which limits their deployment on resource-constrained devices. In this work, we present NanoSleep, a compact hybrid temporal convolutional network for automatic sleep stage classification. NanoSleep combines a learnable Sinc-convolutional front end, a dual-branch feature extractor that fuses multi-scale temporal and spectral representations, a gated dilated temporal convolutional backbone with channel recalibration, and a conditional random field for sequence-level decoding. We further employ a weighted calibrated focal loss to address class imbalance. We evaluate NanoSleep on the Sleep-EDF and Sleep-EDF-Expanded datasets using subject-wise cross-validation. The proposed model consistently outperforms six representative baseline methods, and an ablation study confirms the contribution of each major component. These results demonstrate that NanoSleep provides an effective balance between accuracy and efficiency, making it well suited for wearable devices, home-based sleep monitoring, and resource-constrained clinical applications.

</details>


### [115] [Continual Reasoning Gym: Diagnosing and Harnessing Shared Reasoning in Continual RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18574)
*Lirui Luo,Guoxi Zhang,Hongming Xu,Rongqing Li,Cong Fang,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究持续强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR），提出持续推理健身房环境，发现顺序RLVR存在适度遗忘且最终性能低于多任务RLVR（MTRL）；遗忘仅占性能差距部分，共享推理结构可迁移。提出持续提示重放（CPR），通过重放历史任务提示并用当前策略重新生成响应，利用共享推理提升新任务与未来任务学习，平均达到MTRL水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR通常在多任务上联合后训练推理模型，新增任务需重新运行多任务RLVR（MTRL），成本高昂。因此研究持续RLVR：随着任务到达逐步更新模型。核心问题是持续更新的模型能否达到联合训练的性能。

Method: 构建持续推理健身房（Continual Reasoning Gym），将文本和视觉推理任务组织为五个任务序列。分析顺序RLVR的遗忘与性能差距，分解最终性能发现遗忘仅占部分。识别共享推理：可迁移的推理结构使一个任务的训练平均支持其他任务。提出持续提示重放（CPR）：重放先前任务的提示，并用当前策略重新生成响应，以利用共享推理改善到达任务和未来任务的学习。

Result: 在持续推理健身房五个任务序列上，顺序RLVR表现出适度遗忘，最终性能低于MTRL。遗忘仅解释部分差距。CPR平均达到MTRL级别的性能，显著优于基线。共享推理结构被证实为跨任务迁移的关键。

Conclusion: 持续RLVR中，简单顺序训练不足以匹配联合训练性能；遗忘非唯一瓶颈，共享推理结构至关重要。CPR通过重放历史提示并用当前策略再生成，有效利用共享推理，实现持续学习中达到多任务联合训练的性能，为低成本能力扩展提供可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) commonly post-trains reasoning models on multiple tasks, while rerunning multitask RLVR (MTRL) as new tasks are added makes capability expansion costly. We therefore study continual RLVR, which updates the existing model as each task arrives. The central question is whether a model updated this way can perform as well as a jointly trained model. To answer this question, we introduce Continual Reasoning Gym, a continual-RLVR environment that organizes text and visual reasoning tasks into five task sequences. In this setting, we identify two key observations: Sequential RLVR exhibits modest forgetting, yet its final performance remains below that of MTRL. To understand the latter, we decompose final performance and show that forgetting accounts for only part of the gap. To explain the former, we identify shared reasoning: transferable reasoning structure allows training on one task to support others on average. We therefore introduce Continual Prompt Replay (CPR), which harnesses shared reasoning to improve learning on the arriving and future tasks by replaying previous-task prompts and regenerating their responses with the current policy. On average, only CPR reaches MTRL-level performance.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond receptive fields: sequence-pooled normalization can supply most of a sequence labeler's context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18576)
*Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示卷积序列标注模型中的归一化层在推理时会利用整个序列的统计量，打破卷积感受野的局限，提供全局上下文；这种机制在长程标签任务中贡献巨大，且会干扰消融实验对感受野贡献的归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将卷积感受野视为模型可用上下文范围的假设可能失效：归一化层（如 LayerNorm、BatchNorm）在推理时沿序列维度计算统计量，会开辟一条跨越感受野的全序列信息通路，导致模型实际上下文远超理论感受野。

Method: 从归一化层的雅可比矩阵推导出全序列信息通路的存在及其闭式表达；构建合成标注任务（可计算最优解）量化该通路贡献；在模拟基因组与真实 1000 Genomes 单倍型上对比“关闭通路”（逐位置统计）与“扩大感受野”的效果；通过消融实验与从头重训对比，揭示归因偏差。

Result: 在长程标签任务中，感受野仅 9 位的网络借助归一化通路已达全序列最优的 0.009 差距，而同感受野的无通路基线接近随机猜测；关闭通路后，扩大感受野的收益提升达一个数量级；消融实验会高估感受野扩展模块贡献 8.3–16.1 倍；标签切换频繁时该效应减弱。

Conclusion: 归一化层在推理时引入的全序列统计量是长程依赖任务中真正的关键组件，而非感受野扩展；现有的感受野论证与消融归因在数值上并不错误，但将功劳错误地归因给了卷积架构而非归一化机制。

Abstract: A convolutional sequence labeler's receptive field is routinely treated as the extent of the model's usable context: it sets dilation schedules, bounds streaming horizons, and underwrites locality claims. However, we show that this can be false: when a normalization layer computes statistics from the current input along the sequence at inference, those statistics open a sequence-spanning path that bypasses the convolutional receptive field to provide global context. We derive this from the layer's Jacobian (the criterion needs no experiment), and what the path carries has a closed form. On a synthetic labeling process with computable optima, the global summary that a sequence-spanning normalization encodes already supplies almost all of what a larger receptive field would buy where labels come in long runs: a network reaching 9 positions comes within 0.009 of the whole-sequence optimum, against a near-chance bound for its reach. Closing the path, by taking the same statistics per position, multiplies what enlarging the receptive field is worth by up to an order of magnitude on simulated genomes at every difficulty level tested and on real 1000 Genomes haplotypes. The same path also confounds attribution: ablating a trained network's receptive-field-enlarging blocks severs part of the path, overstating their contribution 8.3-16.1-fold relative to retraining from scratch. The substitution of normalization for receptive field fades as labels switch more often. Where labels run long, neither the receptive-field justification nor the ablation is wrong about its numbers, but both credit the wrong component.

</details>


### [117] [Infrared Universality of Collective Dynamics across Transformer and State-Space Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18592)
*Byung Gyu Chae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨不同神经架构（Transformer 与 Mamba）是否发展出共同的集体动力学。通过分析 Mamba 的三层弛豫动力学谱系，发现尽管微观机制截然不同，Mamba 完整模块仍形成可复现的慢模态连续谱，其红外指数 β_M ≃ -0.17，记忆动力学接近边际 1/t 制度；Transformer 完整模块谱系呈现相似的红外组织（β_Tr ∼ -0.1）。结果表明：显式状态空间记忆与集体红外组织可分离，不同序列架构均能演化出近边际慢模态动力学，并为认知场论提供独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放问题：不同神经架构是否会发展出共同的集体动力学？Transformer 语言模型展现出近平坦、弱红外增强的时间尺度态密度（TDOS），对应近边际长记忆动力学。Mamba 基于选择性状态空间动力学，提供了根本不同的微观机制，因此成为测试类似红外组织是否普适的理想对象。

Method: 利用 Mamba 可解析的三层弛豫动力学：① 学习到的状态空间生成器的本征谱；② 输入条件化的选择性重缩放；③ 从完整模块 Jacobian 测得的集体 TDOS。对比三者谱系差异，累积分析得 ρ(λ)∼λ^β，推导记忆核 K(t)∼t^{-(1+β)}，并随序列长度考察红外区收敛；同步测量 Transformer 完整模块谱系进行横向比较。

Result: 选择性动力学与模块内其余变换大幅重组微观弛豫层级，但完整模块仍涌现可复现的慢模态连续谱，红外区随序列增长渐进清晰。长序列 Mamba 指数稳定在 β_M ≃ -0.17，记忆动力学接近边际 1/t；Transformer 完整模块红外指数 β_Tr ∼ -0.1，两者红外组织高度相似。

Conclusion: 显式状态空间记忆与集体红外组织可分离；截然不同的序列架构均能自发发展出近边际的慢模态集体动力学。红外集体组织不局限于 Transformer，且为认知场论的动力学结构提供了独立实验验证。

Abstract: Whether distinct neural architectures develop common collective dynamics remains an open question. Recent analysis of Transformer language models revealed a nearly flat, weakly infrared-enhanced time-scale density of states (TDOS) associated with near-marginal long-memory dynamics. Here we test whether a closely related organization emerges in Mamba, whose selective state-space dynamics provides a fundamentally different microscopic mechanism. Mamba allows relaxation dynamics to be resolved at three levels: the intrinsic spectrum of the learned state-space generator, its input-conditioned selective rescaling, and the collective TDOS of the complete block measured from its Jacobian. These spectra are not identical: selective dynamics and the remaining block transformations substantially reorganize the microscopic relaxation hierarchy. Nevertheless, the full block develops a reproducible slow-mode continuum whose infrared sector becomes progressively better resolved with increasing sequence length. Cumulative analysis yields $ρ(λ)\simλ^β$, with the long-sequence Mamba exponent stabilizing near $β_{\rm M}\simeq-0.17$. The corresponding memory dynamics follows $K(t)\sim t^{-(1+β)}$, close to the marginal $1/t$ regime. Despite fundamentally different microscopic dynamics, Transformer full-block spectra exhibit closely related infrared organization, with representative exponents of order $β_{\rm Tr}\sim-0.1$. These results separate explicit state-space memory from collective infrared organization and show that distinct sequence architectures can develop closely related near-marginal slow-mode dynamics. They extend infrared collective organization beyond Transformers and provide an independent test of the dynamical structure described by Cognitive Field Theory.

</details>


### [118] [Off-Manifold Collapse in Guided Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18597)
*Shuibai Zhang,Xinchi Liu,Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Zhihan Yang,Shutong Wu,Yingzi Ma,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对蛋白质语言模型在推理时引导生成序列时出现的“off-manifold collapse”现象，提出了一种无需训练的后处理方法——马哈拉诺比斯过滤，通过在自然蛋白激活空间建立廉价密度先验，过滤掉偏离流形的生成序列，从而在不修改生成器的情况下提升属性得分和结构合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型在推理时引导生成面临两难：温和引导难以改变目标属性，强引导虽能优化属性但导致生成序列偏离自然蛋白流形，变得难以折叠且低复杂度，且属性预言模型可能错误地给予高分（如溶解度预言模型甚至奖励这种崩塌）。现有方法多在生成器层面修正，本文旨在从输出端检测并过滤崩塌序列。

Method: 提出 Mahalanobis filtering（马哈拉诺比斯过滤），即在自然蛋白激活空间上拟合一个廉价的密度先验（高斯分布），计算生成序列激活的马哈拉诺比斯距离，仅保留距离阈值内的典型候选。这是一个无需训练、即插即用的后处理步骤，可迁移至不同引导方法。

Result: 在匹配引导强度下，该过滤显著提升保留序列的属性得分（如溶解度）和结构合理性（如 pLDDT），计算开销极小，且不接触生成器参数。实验表明该方法能有效识别并剔除 off-manifold collapse 的序列。

Conclusion: 蛋白质语言模型推理引导的失败表现为激活空间的流形崩塌，可通过输出端的统计检测廉价解决。马哈拉诺比斯过滤作为一种通用的即插即用后处理，能在不修改生成器的前提下同时提升属性优化效果和结构可信度，为蛋白质序列设计提供了实用的安全机制。

Abstract: Protein language models are widely used priors for protein sequence design, and a growing body of work controls them at inference time as an alternative to fine-tuning. Such guidance faces a dilemma: mild enough to preserve natural activation statistics, it barely moves the property; strong enough to move it, the generations become progressively harder to fold. We show the failure has a specific and cheaply detectable signature, an off-manifold collapse of the model's own representations. Guided activations fall toward a region statistically indistinguishable from random amino-acid input, and the sequences degenerate to low complexity, yet the property oracle being optimized can still score these generations as a success. The optimized oracle can therefore fail to witness the collapse and, for solubility, can actively reward it, whereas structure and composition expose the failure. Because the failure is already visible in a finished candidate, we detect it at the output rather than modify the generator. We introduce a cheap density prior over natural protein activations and keep only the candidates that remain typical under it, a training-free post-hoc step we call Mahalanobis filtering. At matched guidance settings it improves both the property score and the structural plausibility of the sequences it keeps at negligible cost, without touching the generator, and transfers across different guidance methods. We release the activation statistic at https://huggingface.co/Shuibai12138/off-manifold-collapse-plm

</details>


### [119] [Denoising-Aware Inversion: Revealing Privacy Risks in Noise-Protected Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18610)
*Yubo Wang,Shujie Cui,James Bailey,Hongzhi Yin,Wenyu Liang,Min Tang,Shiyue Qin,Weiqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究文本嵌入反转攻击在高斯噪声保护下的有效性，指出现有生成式反转方法因“双重噪声陷阱”而失效，提出基于无监督去噪自编码器的DAEI流水线，实验显示其显著提升重构质量，挑战了简单高斯扰动足以保护隐私的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集文本嵌入广泛用于数据挖掘、检索和下游机器学习系统，但近期嵌入反转攻击表明其可能泄露原始文本信息。常见防御是添加高斯噪声发布扰动嵌入，这对标准反转攻击有效且不显著降低下游任务效用。然而，尚不清楚这种噪声保护的嵌入是否足以抵御显式考虑扰动过程的自适应攻击者。

Method: 提出DAEI（去噪感知嵌入反转）流水线，结合残差去噪自编码器与生成式文本反转。去噪器采用无监督方式训练，利用Stein无偏风险估计（SURE）仅从噪声观测中实现去噪，从而解决“双重噪声陷阱”问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，DAEI在BLEU指标上比现有生成式反转基线实现约154%的相对提升，Token级F1和ROUGE-L分别提升32%-60%，展示了在噪声保护设置下的优异反转性能。

Conclusion: DAEI的成功挑战了主流假设，即简单的高斯扰动足以防止嵌入表示中的敏感信息泄露，表明需要更强的隐私保护机制。

Abstract: Dense text embeddings are widely used in data mining, retrieval, and downstream machine learning systems due to their compact and semantically rich representations, but recent embedding inversion attacks have shown that they can expose substantial information about the original text, leading to serious privacy leakage risks. A common defense is to release perturbed embeddings by adding Gaussian noise, which is simple yet effective against standard inversion attacks and does not significantly degrade embedding utility for downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether such noise-protected embeddings are sufficiently safe against adaptive attackers that explicitly account for the perturbation process. In this paper, we study text embedding inversion in a noise-protected setting, where the attacker can observe only noisy embeddings and has no access to clean embedding targets. We first analyze why existing generative inversion methods fail under this setting and identify a "Double Noise Trap", which fundamentally prevents standard generative inversion models from achieving high-quality reconstruction. To address this challenge, we propose DAEI, a denoising-aware embedding inversion pipeline that combines a residual denoising autoencoder with generative text inversion where the denoiser is trained in an unsupervised manner using Stein's unbiased risk estimate to enable denoising from noisy observations alone. Extensive experiments show that DAEI achieves approximately 154\% relative improvement in BLEU over the existing generative inversion baseline, while also improving token-level F1 and ROUGE-L by 32--60\%. The promising inversion performance of DAEI challenges the prevailing assumption that simple Gaussian perturbation is sufficient to prevent sensitive information leakage from embedding representations.

</details>


### [120] [Scalable Geospatial Machine Learning for Power-Line Asset Risk: Integrating Remote Sensing for Lightning and Vegetation Risk Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18611)
*Artur Sokolovsky,Bhavik Merai,Moe Jafari,Muen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种模块化、鲁棒且可解释的故障概率（PoF）建模框架，用于电力资产管理，能够整合多源地理空间数据，实现资产级风险分层，支持检查优先级排序、植被管理、资产加固和韧性规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力网络面临日益增加的天气敏感故障机制，需要资产级、空间显性的风险建模以制定有效的干预计划。现有模型需在保持运营可维护性的同时，适应不断变化的数据可用性、资产管理优先级和气候驱动的危害条件。

Method: 构建了一个统一的地理空间机器学习管道，整合多源预测因子：地形（SRTM）、植被状况（MODIS NDVI）、闪电气候学（LIS VHRMC）、OpenStreetMap衍生的邻近特征以及公用事业运营记录。采用模块化架构，可扩展至新环境数据源和额外PoF类型，无需重构底层管道。

Result: 该框架计算效率高、运营可扩展、适合公用事业规模部署。为植被相关和闪电相关故障模式提供了可操作的资产级风险分层，支持更早干预和更具气候韧性的网络运营。

Conclusion: 提出的PoF建模框架实现了模块化、鲁棒性和可解释性，能够适应不断变化的数据和气候条件，为电力资产管理提供了实用的风险分层工具，有助于提升电网的气候适应能力和运营韧性。

Abstract: Electric power networks are increasingly exposed to weather-sensitive failure mechanisms that require asset-level, spatially explicit risk modelling for effective intervention planning. This study contributes a modular, robust, and explainable probability-of-failure (PoF) modelling framework for utility asset management. The central contribution is an asset-level architecture that can be scaled to new environmental data sources and additional PoF types without reworking the underlying pipeline. This is particularly relevant for industry settings, where risk models must remain operationally maintainable while adapting to changing data availability, asset-management priorities, and climate-driven hazard conditions. We demonstrate the framework for vegetation-related and lightning-related failure modes using a harmonised geospatial machine-learning pipeline. The implementation integrates multi-source predictors, including topography (SRTM), vegetation condition (MODIS Normalised Difference Vegetation Index - NDVI), lightning climatology (LIS VHRMC), OpenStreetMap-derived proximity features, and utility operational records. The resulting architecture is computationally efficient, operationally extensible, and suitable for utility-scale deployment. It provides actionable asset-level risk stratification for inspection prioritisation, vegetation management, asset hardening, and resilience planning, supporting earlier intervention and more climate-resilient network operations.

</details>


### [121] [Coordination on a Budget: Federated Active Learning with Few Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18634)
*Liam Mohr,Daphna Weinshall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究联邦主动学习（FAL）在低预算场景下的异质性反转现象，提出基于联邦表征学习的框架，实现服务器端全局协调查询选择，在低预算下超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决联邦主动学习中数据隐私与标签稀缺的双重挑战，特别是低预算场景下协调查询选择的困难；发现异质性反转现象：低预算下同质（IID）数据需更强协调避免冗余查询，而异质数据天然促进多样性，这与标准联邦学习中异质性是主要挑战的叙事相反。

Method: 提出新FAL框架，利用联邦表征学习将客户端数据对齐到共享嵌入空间，服务器在可选混淆的客户端嵌入上进行全局协调主动选择，标注保持在本地。

Result: 在更具挑战性的低预算场景下，该框架性能超越现有FAL方法，即使后者拥有大得多的标注预算，证明了隐私约束下中心化协调的价值。

Conclusion: 联邦主动学习中低预算场景下IID设置比异质设置更具挑战性，通过联邦表征学习实现共享嵌入空间的全局协调查询选择能有效解决这一问题，且在隐私保护前提下优于现有方法。

Abstract: Federated Active Learning (FAL) addresses the dual challenges of data privacy and label scarcity, where the absence of a global data view introduces additional hurdles for coordinated query selection. We study cross-silo FAL in the low-budget regime, where annotation decisions are most critical. We characterize, both theoretically and empirically, a heterogeneity reversal: in low-budget settings, homogeneous (IID) data requires stronger coordination to avoid redundant queries, whereas heterogeneous data naturally promotes diversity; this trend reverses at higher budgets. Thus, in contrast to the standard federated learning (FL) narrative where heterogeneity is a primary challenge, we show that IID settings are more challenging for query selection in FAL.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose a new FAL framework that utilizes federated representation learning to align client data in a shared embedding space. This enables the server to perform globally coordinated active selection over optionally obfuscated client embeddings, while annotation remains local to each client. Although our framework operates in the more challenging low-budget regime, it achieves performance that surpasses existing FAL methods even when they are given substantially larger annotation budgets, demonstrating the value of centralized coordination under privacy constraints.

</details>


### [122] [ProxyGuard: Direct Reliability Inference for Randomized Data Release Mechanisms with Shared Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18643)
*Dipesh Tharu Mahato,Pramod Dhungana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProxyGuard 提出了一种统计框架，用于在研究者从多个候选版本（releases）中选择代理数据集时，同时控制两类错误：搜索导致无效版本看似合格，以及单一合格版本无法证明生成器可靠。该方法包含两种模式：Named-release 模式校正多重性并认证特定版本；Direct shared-target 模式在共同目标上评估独立机制抽样，给出有限样本下的机制可靠性保证，无需独立目标批次或释放层面 p 值依赖假设。实验显示 Direct 模式在可靠性 0.95 下将功效从 5.6% 提升至 64.2%，Named 模式在强信号下更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常从多个发布版本、变换或随机种子中挑选代理数据集，但现有方法无法同时解决：(1) 搜索过程使无效版本通过测试的假阳性风险；(2) 仅观察到一个合格版本不足以证实生成机制的整体可靠性。需要一种能给出有限样本、无强依赖假设的双重错误控制框架。

Method: ProxyGuard 使用预先指定的有界风险和封闭目标集。两种模式：Named-release 模式通过多重性校正认证特定版本；Direct shared-target 模式将多个独立机制抽样在同一目标上评分，利用条件独立性给出有利评分率的下界，并扣除无效版本贡献的上界，得到机制可靠性的有限样本保证。理论上证明了仅均值惩罚的锐度，并推导了带加性目标集中项的平滑评分证书。

Result: 在一个预注册的三要求研究中，Direct 模式在可靠性 0.95 下将统计功效从 5.6% 大幅提升至 64.2%，而 Named 模式在高信号证据下表现更强。前瞻性审计覆盖了全流程 Rice-TVAE（每次抽样重新训练）和非表格文本生成机制，验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: ProxyGuard 为代理数据集选择提供了严格的统计保证，同时控制选择偏差和机制可靠性推断的不确定性。其 Direct 模式在低信号场景下显著提升功效，Named 模式在强信号下更优，且不依赖独立目标批次或 p 值依赖假设，适用于完整生成流水线的前瞻性审计。

Abstract: Researchers often choose a proxy dataset from many releases, transformations, or seeds. Search can make an invalid release appear adequate, while one adequate release does not establish that its generator is reliable. ProxyGuard controls both errors using prespecified bounded risks and a sealed target set. Named-release mode corrects for multiplicity and certifies specific releases. Direct shared-target mode evaluates independent mechanism draws on a common target, lower-bounds their favorable-score rate, and subtracts a bound on favorable scores contributed by invalid releases. Conditional on the target, release scores are independent, yielding a finite-sample mechanism-reliability guarantee without independent target batches or assumptions on release-level $p$-value dependence. We show that the mean-only penalty is sharp and derive a smooth-score certificate with additive target concentration. In a registered three-requirement study, direct mode raises power from 5.6\% to 64.2\% at reliability 0.95, while named mode remains stronger under high-signal evidence. Prospective audits span full-pipeline Rice--TVAE, which retrains on every draw, and a non-tabular text mechanism.

</details>


### [123] [FlashAttention for Scalable Vector Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18656)
*Sonia Rani Gupta,Nikela Papadopoulou,Miquel Pericàs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlashAttention-V，一种面向可扩展向量架构的分块FlashAttention，通过跨注意力头并行性、头间打包及向量寄存器利用率优化，在RISC-V向量扩展(RVV)和Arm SVE上实现显著加速。实验表明预填充阶段最高达42倍加速，解码阶段8-11倍加速，但量化线性层的结构瓶颈限制了长向量扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在CPU上的推理日益重要，特别是小语言模型(SLMs)，向量架构成为有前景的执行底层。注意力模块因高内存带宽需求成为主要瓶颈；FlashAttention通过算子融合改善数据局部性和减少中间内存流量来缓解此问题。需要一种能高效适应从短向量到极长向量的FlashAttention变体。

Method: 提出FlashAttention-V：一种面向可扩展向量架构的分块FlashAttention。核心技术包括：1)利用注意力头间并行性；2)头间打包以实现超越头维度的向量长度高效利用；3)改进向量寄存器利用率和内存访问局部性。将FlashAttention-V集成到llama.cpp的ggml中，在TinyLlama、Llama 3.2、Qwen2.5和Pythia-410M上使用gem5仿真器和Banana Pi BPI-F3开发板进行评估。

Result: 在Banana Pi BPI-F3上验证循环重排和跨注意力头循环展开为有效优化原则，性能增益随模型规模增大而提升，在短上下文和解码阶段最显著。仿真分析显示：预填充阶段，512位向量长度下比标量FlashAttention快22-42倍，扩展到64通道和4096位向量长度额外获得2-2.5倍加速。解码阶段，512位向量长度下获得8-11倍加速，性能对向量宽度和通道数敏感度降低，因单token内存受限执行。进一步发现Q8_0量化线性层存在结构瓶颈，限制长向量执行下的算术摊销，这在RVV和Arm SVE上一致，表明当前量化格式对长向量扩展性构成根本挑战。

Conclusion: FlashAttention-V通过跨头并行、头间打包和寄存器优化，成功将FlashAttention适配到可扩展向量架构，在预填充和解码阶段均实现显著加速。然而，量化线性层(Q8_0)的结构瓶颈限制了长向量执行下的算术强度摊销，揭示了当前量化格式对长向量可扩展性的根本性挑战，未来工作需探索向量友好的量化格式。

Abstract: Inference with transformer models on CPUs is increasingly important, especially for Small Language Models (SLMs), where vector architectures are emerging as a promising execution substrate. The attention module is a major bottleneck due to high memory bandwidth requirements; FlashAttention mitigates this by fusing operations to improve data locality and reduce intermediate memory traffic. In this paper, we present FlashAttention-V, a blocked FlashAttention for scalable vector architectures that adapts efficiently from short to very long vectors by exploiting parallelism across attention heads, inter-head packing to enable efficient utilization of vector lengths beyond the head dimension, and improving vector register utilization and memory access locality. We integrate FlashAttention-V into ggml within llama.cpp and evaluate it on TinyLlama, Llama 3.2, Qwen2.5, and Pythia-410M using gem5 and a Banana Pi BPI-F3. On the Banana Pi BPI-F3, we confirm that loop reordering and loop unrolling across attention heads are effective optimization principles, scaling performance gains with larger models and most pronounced with short contexts and during decoding. Simulation-based analysis shows that FlashAttention-V achieves 22x-42x speedup over scalar FlashAttention at 512-bit VL in prefill, with an additional 2x-2.5x gain scaling to 64 lanes and 4096-bit VL. During decode, FlashAttention-V achieves 8x-11x speedup using 512-bit vector lengths over scalar FlashAttention, with performance showing diminishing sensitivity to vector width and lane count due to single-token, memory-bound execution. We further identify structural bottlenecks in Q8_0 quantized linear layers that limit arithmetic amortization under long-vector execution, consistent across RVV and Arm SVE, indicating that current quantization formats pose a fundamental challenge to long-vector scalability.

</details>


### [124] [Computational Measurement of Team-Process Phase Dynamics in Collaborative Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18660)
*Qing Huang,Jianing Zhang,Pooja Pol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算框架，用于从协作VR游戏的时间戳对话中检测和解释动态团队过程阶段，结合晚期分块、变点检测、TF-IDF、NMF及大语言模型，实现了连贯且可解释的阶段结构识别，并验证了其与交互行为的对应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的协作VR环境中团队沟通分析常对整个试验汇总或划分为固定时间窗口，难以捕捉团队沟通和协调随时间的动态变化，因此需要一种能够自动检测和解释动态团队过程阶段的方法。

Method: 框架采用晚期分块生成上下文感知的转录表示，聚合为时间块，应用惩罚高斯核变点检测识别语义转换；利用TF-IDF、非负矩阵分解（NMF）和代表性转录片段提供结构化证据；本地部署大语言模型通过上下文学习生成初步解释，经人工复核；再将独立记录的交互日志与检测阶段对齐，分析任务行动模式。

Result: 评估表明该框架能识别连贯且可解释的阶段结构，并保留对底层转录证据的可追溯性；基于转录的阶段与交互行为的对应性进一步支持其在协作活动分析中的相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架为从时间戳转录文本中研究团队协作的时间动态变化提供了一种透明且可迁移的方法，适用于各类协作任务场景。

Abstract: Collaborative virtual reality (VR) environments make team communication observable as it unfolds, but conventional transcript analyses often summarize entire trials or divide them into fixed temporal windows. Such approaches can obscure changes in team communication and coordination over time. This article presents a computational framework for detecting and interpreting dynamic team-process phases from timestamped dialogue in a collaborative VR game. The framework uses late chunking to generate context-aware transcript representations, aggregates them into temporal chunks, and applies penalized Gaussian-kernel change-point detection to identify semantic transitions in team communication. After boundary detection, term frequency--inverse document frequency (TF-IDF), non-negative matrix factorization (NMF), and representative transcript segments provide structured evidence for phase interpretation. A locally deployed large language model (LLM) uses in-context learning to generate initial interpretations that are subsequently reviewed by humans. Independently recorded interaction logs are then aligned with the detected phases to examine corresponding task-action patterns. The evaluation compares representations, pooling strategies, segmentation methods, parameter settings, reviewed phase interpretations, and phase-aligned interaction profiles. The results show that the framework identifies coherent and interpretable phase structures while preserving traceability to the underlying transcript evidence. The correspondence between transcript-derived phases and interaction behavior further supports their relevance for analyzing collaborative activity. The framework therefore offers a transparent and transferable approach for studying temporal changes in teamwork from timestamped transcripts across collaborative task settings.

</details>


### [125] [Reinforced Planning with Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18669)
*Armin Sommer,Jannik Schilling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了强化规划（RP1），一种完全学习如何改进多步计划的方法，通过离线从想象的世界模型轨迹中训练评论家和优化器，实现了比手工设计搜索算法显著更好的性能，且使用少1000倍的世界模型推理和快67倍的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过构建计划并用内部世界模型模拟结果来解决复杂问题。机器学习已产生能预测动作序列结果的世界模型，但候选计划的改进仍未完全学习。现有规划器要么是手工设计，要么从手工优化器蒸馏，要么仅学习指导摊销策略而非修订计划本身。

Method: 强化规划（RP1）基于通过强化好的搜索规则到神经规划器中来学习搜索的思想。其实现学习如何通过评论家评估想象结果，以及如何通过完全从想象的世界模型轨迹离线训练的优化器来改进多步计划。RP1可独立于任何预训练潜在世界模型进行训练并附加其上。

Result: 在视觉导航、机械臂触达和机器人操作任务上，使用两种世界模型骨干网络，RP1大幅优于手工设计的搜索算法，在多个设置中达到近乎完美的成功率，同时使用1000倍更少的世界模型推理，并在并发规划器推理下比最强替代方案快达67倍。

Conclusion: RP1是首个完全学习如何改进多步计划的方法，展示了通过强化学习方式训练神经规划器进行搜索的有效性，为基于世界模型的规划提供了可扩展、高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Humans solve complex problems by constructing plans and mentally simulating their outcomes with an internal model of the world. Machine learning has produced world models that similarly predict the outcomes of action sequences, but the improvement of candidate plans still isn't fully learned. Current planners are either hand-designed, distilled from a hand-designed optimizer, or learned only to inform an amortized policy rather than to revise the plan itself. We introduce the Reinforced Planning, a method based on the idea that search can be learned by reinforcing good search rules into a neural planner. Our implementation RP1 learns both how to evaluate imagined outcomes through a critic, as well as how to improve multi-step plans through an optimizer trained fully offline from imagined world-model roll-outs. To our knowledge, RP1 is the first method to fully learn how to improve multi-step plans. Furthermore, it can be trained independently of and attached to any pretrained latent world model. Across visual navigation, arm reaching, and robotic manipulation on two world-model backbones, RP1 substantially outperforms hand-designed search algorithms, reaching near-perfect success in several settings while using $1,000 \times$ less world-model rollouts and being up to $67 \times$ faster than the strongest alternative under concurrent planner inference.

</details>


### [126] [An Empirical Benchmark of Deep Time-Series Models for Smart Meter Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18675)
*Behnaz Kavoosighafi,Maria Eidenskog,Wiktoria Glad,Katerina Vrotsou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在两个公开智能电表数据集上对比了九种现代深度学习时序预测模型（线性、MLP、卷积、Transformer），系统研究了历史输入长度、预测步长、模型架构对预测精度的影响，并分析了精度与计算复杂度的权衡、统计显著性及实际效用差异，最后通过人口统计学子群分析发现模型选择对大多数人群段影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的能耗预测对电力系统高效运行至关重要，智能电表提供了大量高分辨率数据，数据驱动方法被广泛应用，但现代深度学习模型在真实智能电表数据上的相对性能尚未充分研究，需要系统的实证基准。

Method: 选取九种代表性深度学习架构（线性、MLP-based、卷积、Transformer），在两个公开智能电表数据集上进行短期与长期预测实验，重点考察历史输入长度、预测步长、模型架构三个因素；同时评估计算复杂度、统计显著性及实际效用差异，并按地理人口统计与家庭类别进行子群分析。

Result: 1) 增加历史输入长度可提升精度，但存在饱和点，超过后边际收益有限；2) 预测步长越长精度下降越明显；3) 深度学习模型整体优于经典基线，轻量级架构在极低计算成本下达到相近性能；4) 架构差异仅在长步长预测和更异质数据集上显现；5) 子群分析显示模型选择对大多数人群段的实际影响有限。

Conclusion: 实证基准表明：现代深度学习模型在智能电表能耗预测中表现优异，轻量级模型具有极高性价比；历史上下文长度和预测步长是关键超参数，架构选择的重要性随预测步长和数据异质性增加；实际部署时，针对大多数用户群体，简单轻量模型已足够，无需过度追求复杂架构。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of energy consumption is important for the efficient operation of power systems, with direct implications for operational costs, energy management, and system maintenance. Due to the availability of extensive high-resolution consumption data from smart meters, data-driven methods have been used for short-term and long-term forecasting. However, their comparative performance on real-world smart meter data is still not well studied. In this paper, we present an empirical benchmark of nine modern deep learning models for time-series forecasting, including linear, MLP-based, convolutional, and Transformer architectures. We evaluate these models on two publicly available smart meter datasets. Our analysis focuses on three factors that strongly affect forecasting performance: the length of historical input, the prediction horizon, and the choice of model architecture. We show that extending the historical context improves accuracy, but only up to a saturation point, after which additional input provides limited benefit. In contrast, accuracy decreases as the prediction horizon increases. We also investigate the trade-off between prediction accuracy and computational complexity, and assess the statistical significance and practical magnitude of performance differences across models. Our results show that deep learning models consistently outperform classical baselines, while lightweight architectures achieve relatively similar performance at significantly lower computational cost. Additionally, architectural differences only become meaningful at longer forecasting horizons and on more heterogeneous datasets. Finally, a subgroup analysis across geodemographic and household categories shows that model choice has limited impact for most population segments.

</details>


### [127] [Transforming Heart Disease Prediction with Advanced Machine Learning Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18687)
*Sami Ullah,Muhammad Mohsin Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习技术对比多种分类器在两个心脏病数据集上的表现，发现SVM在UCI数据集上最优，Simple Cart在Kaggle数据集上最优，证明ML模型可辅助心脏病早期诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏病是全球主要死亡原因，早期准确检测对改善患者预后至关重要，因此需要利用机器学习进行预测分析。

Method: 使用UCI和Kaggle两个含14个属性的心脏病数据集，应用J48、朴素贝叶斯、逻辑回归、Simple Cart、Bagging、Decision Stump、AdaBoost、人工神经网络、SVM等分类器，采用MAE、RAE、准确率、精确率、召回率、F值等指标评估性能。

Result: SVM在UCI数据集上达到最高性能，Simple Cart在Kaggle数据集上表现最佳，具有最高准确率和最低错误率。

Conclusion: 经过适当调优和验证的机器学习模型能显著辅助心脏病早期诊断，为临床决策提供关键支持；未来工作可探索混合方法和更新数据集以进一步提升预测准确性。

Abstract: Heart disease remains the leading cause of mortality globally, necessitating early and accurate detection to improve patient outcomes. This research focuses on the predictive analysis of heart disease using machine learning (ML) techniques, comparing the performance of multiple classifiers to identify the most accurate and least error-prone method. Two datasets from UCI and Kaggle repositories were utilized, each containing 14 attributes related to heart health indicators. Techniques including J48, Naive Bayes, Logistic Regression, Simple Cart, Bagging, Decision Stump, AdaBoost, Artificial Neural Networks, and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were applied. Evaluation metrics such as Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Relative Absolute Error (RAE), accuracy, precision, recall, and F-measure were used for performance comparison. Results revealed that SVM achieved the highest performance on the UCI dataset, while Simple Cart performed best on the Kaggle dataset, offering the highest accuracy and lowest error rates. The research work concludes that ML models, when properly tuned and validated, can significantly assist in the early diagnosis of heart disease, offering critical support for clinical decision-making. Future work may involve hybrid approaches and the use of more recent datasets to further improve prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [128] [Europe's Climate Ambition Under Scrutiny: Evidence from Deep Learning Emission Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18690)
*Jacopo Ghirri,Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Lara Aleluia Reis,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 欧盟2030年减排目标恐难达成：现有趋势下二氧化碳排放将超标35%（缺口6.2亿吨），仅极少数国家轨迹符合承诺；电力部门因可再生能源转型表现较好，但交通部门进展极小，占2030年总排放超三分之一，反映结构性惯性。需大幅强化干预并建立及时能源信息体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟承诺2030年温室气体排放较1990年减少55%，但现有趋势是否兼容该目标尚存不确定性，亟需基于高分辨率数据的定量评估来量化雄心与实施的差距。

Method: 利用深度学习模型，对欧盟27国截至2023年的高分辨率社会经济与部门数据进行建模，外推观测到的部门动量，在不预设政策环境节奏或有效性变化的前提下预测各部门二氧化碳排放轨迹。

Result: 预测欧盟27国2030年排放将超标35%（缺口约6.2亿吨二氧化碳），仅极少数国家轨迹符合集团承诺。电力部门得益于可再生能源转型实现目标一致性减排；交通部门进展微乎其微，2030年将占总排放超1/3，且这种惯性普遍存在于成员国而非集中于特定落后国家。

Conclusion: 研究揭示欧盟存在显著的雄心-实施差距，须大幅度追加干预措施；同时呼吁建立及时更新的欧洲能源信息体系以支撑决策。

Abstract: The European Union has committed to reducing greenhouse gas emissions 55% below 1990 levels by 2030, but whether current trends are compatible with this ambition remains uncertain. We apply deep learning to high-resolution socioeconomic and sectoral data across EU27 member states till 2023 to project sectoral CO$_2$ trajectories under current trends, extrapolating observed sectoral momentum without assuming changes in the pace or effectiveness of the policy environment beyond what is already reflected in historical data. We project that EU27 emissions will exceed the 2030 target by 35% (620 Mt CO$_2$ shortfall), with only a small minority of countries on trajectories consistent with the bloc's commitments. While the Power sector achieves target-consistent reductions driven by the renewable transition, Mobility shows minimal progress and accounts for over a third of total emissions by 2030, reflecting a structural inertia across member states rather than geographically concentrated lag. Our findings indicate that substantial additional intervention is required to close Europe's ambition-implementation gap, and call for establishing up-to-date energy information in Europe.

</details>


### [129] [Multi-Class Electrical and Mechanical Fault Classification Using Random Convolutional Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18716)
*Mouhamadou Mansour Lo,Mouad Talbaoui,Gildas Morvan,Mathieu Rossi,Fabrice Morganti,David Mercier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了 SelF-Rocket 在旋转机械机械与电气故障多类诊断中的表现，并提出了其多变量扩展版本。实验表明该方法在准确率与延迟的权衡上表现最优，在 MaFaulDa 数据集上取得最高分类性能，在更具挑战性的 ITSC-UDG 数据集上也极具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转机械故障诊断对工业可靠性至关重要。基于随机卷积核的时间序列分类方法（如 ROCKET 及其变体）在预测性能与计算效率之间提供了良好折中，但其在多变量故障诊断场景下的表现尚待系统评估与改进。

Method: 提出 SelF-Rocket 的多变量扩展，并在单变量与多变量设置下，于两个公开基准数据集（MaFaulDa 机械故障、ITSC-UDG 定子匝间短路）上与主流 ROCKET 类方法进行对比实验。

Result: SelF-Rocket 在所有评估方法中实现了最佳的整体准确率-延迟权衡；在 MaFaulDa 上获得最高分类性能，在更难的 ITSC-UDG 上仍保持高竞争力。

Conclusion: SelF-Rocket 及其多变量扩展为旋转机械复杂故障诊断提供了高效且高精度的解决方案，适合实际工业部署。

Abstract: Diagnosing faults in rotating machinery is essential for ensuring the reliability of industrial processes. Random convolutional kernel-based Time Series Classification (TSC) methods, such as ROCKET and its variants, provide an attractive trade-off between predictive performance and computational efficiency. In this work, we evaluate SelF-Rocket for the multi-class diagnosis of both mechanical and electrical faults and introduce, as a new contribution, a multivariate extension of the original method. The proposed approach is compared with leading ROCKET-based methods on two public benchmark datasets, MaFaulDa (mechanical faults) and ITSC-UDG (stator inter-turn short circuits), under both univariate and multivariate settings. Experimental results show that SelF-Rocket achieves the best overall accuracy-latency trade-off among the evaluated methods, obtaining the highest classification performance on MaFaulDa while remaining highly competitive on the more challenging ITSC-UDG dataset.

</details>


### [130] [FedLNS: Leverage LayerNorm Signature Modeling to Mitigate Adversarial Manipulation in Federated LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18736)
*Kai Li,Jong-Ik Park,Carlee Joe-Wong,Wei Ni,Falko Dressler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedLNS是一种服务端轻量级恶意更新筛选框架，利用归一化层参数变化作为签名，无需额外通信开销，在40%恶意客户端下优于六个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中服务端无法验证本地监督和优化过程，恶意客户端可通过损坏目标、引入错误上下文-令牌关联降低全局模型性能，增加幻觉风险。

Method: 提出FedLNS，在服务端从返回的本地模型中提取可训练归一化层参数的变化作为签名，基于历史感知的跨客户端参考筛选可疑更新，无需额外客户端到服务端参数或元数据交换，筛选后保留的完整模型更新可用标准FL聚合。

Result: 在GPT、BERT、LLaMA三种架构、200客户端、40%人口级目标操纵下，FedLNS在IID和非IID数据分区上测试困惑度均低于六个最强基线。

Conclusion: FedLNS无需原始客户端数据、可信服务端数据集、标记攻击样本或单独训练的检测器，提供轻量级、有效的恶意更新防御，适用于多种Transformer架构。

Abstract: Federated training enables language models to learn from distributed private text, but the server cannot directly verify the local supervision or optimization process that produces each client update. A malicious client can therefore train on corrupted targets, introduce incorrect context-token associations, and degrade the global model through repeated aggregation. Such degradation can also increase the risk of unreliable or hallucinatory generation. We propose Federated Learning with Normalization Signatures (FedLNS), a server-side framework for lightweight malicious-update screening. FedLNS represents each client update through changes in trainable normalization-layer parameters and screens suspicious updates against a robust, history-aware cross-client reference. Because the signatures are extracted at the server from the returned local models, FedLNS requires no additional client-to-server parameter or metadata exchange compared to standard federated learning (FL) methods. After screening, the retained full-model updates can be aggregated using standard FL or another compatible aggregation rule. FedLNS requires no raw client data, trusted server dataset, labeled attack examples, or separately trained detector. Experiments on GPT-style, BERT-style, and LLaMA-style models trained from scratch with 200 clients show that, under 40% population-level target manipulation, FedLNS achieves lower test perplexity than the strongest of six baselines for all three architectures under both IID (independently and identically distributed) and non-IID data partitions.

</details>


### [131] [Decision-Metric Alignment in Latent World Models: Diagnostics and Action-Conditioned Objectives for MPC Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18746)
*Jiawei Wang,Ke Rui,Yushen Zuo,Yichun Feng,Minglei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出DA-LeWM，通过添加逆动力学和演示条件目标-动作头来改善潜在世界模型中的决策度量对齐，实验显示其加速收敛并提高在线成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA风格的潜在世界模型使用欧氏距离作为MPC成本，但强解码任务变量不保证该成本能按真实任务进度排序候选动作序列，即缺乏“决策度量对齐”。

Method: 引入Plan-Real Spearman和CEM-stage Spearman指标测量潜在-真实排名一致性；分析潜在距离保持真实成本排名的充分条件，识别编码器失真、终端展开误差和候选边界为控制量；提出DA-LeWM，在LeWM基础上增加逆动力学头和演示条件目标-动作头。

Result: 所有实验中DA-LeWM比LeWM收敛更快、在线成功率更高，而探针分数保持相似。

Conclusion: 动作条件目标改善了基于欧氏成本、CEM的潜在MPC所使用的几何结构。

Abstract: JEPA-style latent world models can use Euclidean distance to a goal latent as the cost for model-predictive control (MPC). Strong decoding of task variables, however, does not guarantee that this particular cost ranks candidate action sequences by real task progress. We call the latter property \emph{decision-metric alignment}. We introduce Plan-Real Spearman, which measures latent--real rank agreement on random plans, and CEM-stage Spearman, which measures the same agreement as cross-entropy-method (CEM) search concentrates its proposal. We analyze sufficient conditions under which latent distance preserves real-cost rankings, identifying encoder distortion, terminal rollout error, and candidate margins as the controlling quantities. Guided by the observed empirical alignment gap, DA-LeWM augments LeWM with inverse-dynamics and demonstration-conditioned goal-action heads. Across all our experiments, DA-LeWM accelerates convergence and achieves higher online success than LeWM, while probe scores remain similar. These results show that action-conditioned objectives improve the geometry used by Euclidean-cost, CEM-based latent MPC.

</details>


### [132] [Geometric Data Perturbation with Noisy-Anchor Alignment for Privacy-Preserving Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18749)
*Keiyu Nosaka,Yamato Suetake,Yuichi Takano,Yukihiko Okada,Akiko Yoshise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在锚点表示而非私有数据表示上添加噪声，通过广义正交Procrustes问题对齐表示，在分析师-参与者共谋模型下实现更优的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何数据扰动（GDP）在分析师-参与者共谋下存在隐私泄露风险：参与者独立变换虽能抵抗攻击但导致表示空间不兼容，降低模型性能；共享锚点对齐虽恢复兼容性，但锚点矩阵泄露会导致非共谋参与者私有数据被精确恢复；直接对私有数据加噪虽缓解漏洞但大幅降低效用。

Method: 各参与者独立对私有数据和共享锚点矩阵施加保距变换，仅对锚点表示添加噪声，单轮上传两个表示；分析师利用含噪锚点表示求解广义正交Procrustes问题对齐私有数据表示；理论表征对齐误差、恢复误差，给出收敛的保守充分条件，并分析三种恢复攻击。

Result: 在MNIST和CelebA实验中，锚点加噪策略在同等测量泄露下比私有数据加噪获得更高学习准确率，在指定共谋模型下实现更优隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 锚点表示加噪结合广义正交Procrustes对齐，有效缓解共谋攻击下的隐私泄露，同时保持较高模型效用，为协作学习提供了更优的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Geometric Data Perturbation (GDP) enables one-shot, privacy-preserving collaborative learning: each participant applies a distance-preserving transformation to its private data and uploads only the resulting representation to a central analyst. We study GDP under analyst-participant collusion, in which the analyst combines all uploaded representations with the private data and transformations disclosed by colluding participants to recover a non-colluding participant's private data. Participant-specific independent transformations resist this attack but map participants' data into incompatible representation spaces, degrading downstream model performance. Shared-anchor alignment from Data Collaboration (DC) analysis restores compatibility and improves utility, but we show that disclosing the DC anchor matrix enables exact recovery of non-colluding participants' private data even in the presence of collusion. Adding noise directly to the private-data representations mitigates this vulnerability but substantially reduces utility. We propose adding noise to the anchor representations instead. Each participant independently transforms its private data and the shared anchor matrix, perturbs only the resulting anchor representation, and uploads both representations in a single round. Using the noisy anchor representations, the analyst aligns the private-data representations by solving a Generalized Orthogonal Procrustes Problem. We characterize alignment and recovery errors, specialize a conservative sufficient condition for convergence of the alignment to our setting, and analyze three recovery attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CelebA show that, across the evaluated attacks and deployment settings, anchor noise achieves higher learning accuracy than private-data noise at comparable measured leakage, yielding a more favorable privacy-utility trade-off under the specified collusion model.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Predictive Fairness: Quantifying Attribution Consistency Across Demographic Groups in Diabetic Retinopathy Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18759)
*Kerol Djoumessi,Philipp Berens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解释一致性分数(ECS)基于Jensen-Shannon散度量化不同人口亚组间归因图的相似性,在糖尿病视网膜病变筛选案例研究中发现预测性能跨族群差异但解释一致性较高且与性能差异无显著关联,表明预测公平性与解释一致性捕捉模型行为的互补维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像公平性常通过亚组性能指标评估,但不清楚模型是否依赖跨人口统计学群体一致的视觉证据,需要超越预测性能的公平性评估维度。

Method: 提出基于Jensen-Shannon散度的解释一致性分数(ECS)量化跨亚组归因图相似性,在糖尿病视网膜病变筛选任务中全局及按疾病严重程度评估ECS。

Result: 实验显示预测性能在种族群体间存在差异,但解释一致性保持较高水平且与性能差异无显著关联。

Conclusion: 预测公平性与解释一致性捕捉模型行为的互补维度,激励扩展至预测性能之外的公平性评估。

Abstract: Fairness in medical imaging is commonly evaluated through subgroup performance metrics, yet it remains unclear whether models rely on consistent visual evidence across demographic groups. This work introduces the Explanation Consistency Score (ECS), a fairness-aware metric based on Jensen-Shannon divergence that quantifies the similarity of attribution maps across subgroups. Using diabetic retinopathy screening as a case study, ECS is evaluated globally and within disease severity. Experiments reveal that while predictive performance differs across ethnic groups, explanation consistency remains relatively high and shows no significant association with performance disparities. These findings suggest that predictive fairness and explanation consistency capture complementary dimensions of model behavior, motivating fairness evaluations that extend beyond predictive performance.

</details>


### [134] [Enhancing Distance-Based Graph Autoencoders with Structural Penalties for Dynamic Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18762)
*Aleksandar Tomčić,Miloš Savić,Miloš Radovanović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出三种基于距离的图自编码器变体，引入结构惩罚项（基于度中心性的枢纽惩罚和基于NC-LID的惩罚）到重构损失中，实验表明NC-LID正则化在动态图上持续优于基线和枢纽感知方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图自编码器的优化目标通常忽略节点间的结构异质性，而先前研究表明高NC-LID与嵌入质量下降相关，因此需要将结构信号融入重构损失以提升动态图表示学习效果。

Method: 所有变体共享两层图卷积网络编码器和欧氏距离解码器，基于距离的重构目标。在稀疏校正损失基础上扩展两个节点级正则化项：(i) 基于度中心性的枢纽惩罚；(ii) 基于自然社区局部内在维度(NC-LID)的惩罚，旨在强调结构模糊节点的重构误差。

Result: 在多个动态图数据集上实验表明，引入NC-LID正则化的方法在重构性能上持续优于无结构正则化的基线和使用枢纽感知正则化的方法。

Conclusion: NC-LID作为一种有用的结构信号，能够增强基于距离的图自编码器在动态图设置下的表现，为解决结构异质性问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph autoencoders (GAEs) are widely used for learning representations of dynamic graphs. However, their optimisation objectives typically do not take structural heterogeneity across nodes into account. We propose three distance-based GAE variants that incorporate structural penalties into the reconstruction loss. All variants share a two-layer Graph Convolutional Network encoder and a Euclidean-distance decoder trained with distance-based reconstruction objectives. We extend sparsity-corrected loss with two node-level regularization terms: (i) a hub penalty based on degree centrality, and (ii) a penalty based on Natural Community Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (NC-LID). The paper is motivated by prior evidence linking high NC-LID to reduced embedding quality. The proposed methods are designed to emphasize reconstruction errors for structurally ambiguous nodes. Experiments on multiple dynamic graph data sets show that incorporating NC-LID-based regularization consistently improves reconstruction performance over the baseline without structural regularization and the method using hub-aware regularization. These findings highlight NC-LID as a useful structural signal for enhancing distance-based graph autoencoders in dynamic settings.

</details>


### [135] [To Go Far, Go Together: Diverse Preferences Induce a Curriculum for Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18770)
*Taehyung Kim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CurriPO，一种基于课程学习的树状结构方法，用于解决多用户个性化奖励模型优化中的公平性问题，显著提升群体满意度并减少训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作侧重于提高奖励模型的数据效率和准确性以捕捉少数群体偏好，但忽略了某些用户的奖励模型在策略层面难以优化，导致这些用户成为新的弱势群体。本文观察到不同用户的奖励模型优化难度不同，并在多样化用户群体中自然存在易难课程结构。

Method: 提出 CurriPO，自动构建树状课程结构，从易优化的奖励模型开始，逐步分支并复用已纳入课程的奖励模型，覆盖整个用户群体。这是首个显式利用多用户结构解决 AI 对齐优化问题的工作。

Result: 在模拟环境的个性化连续控制实验中，CurriPO 达到最强基线 1.2–2.1 倍的群体满意度，并大幅减少训练时间。改进主要归因于传统优化忽视的难优化用户群体。

Conclusion: 仅提高奖励模型准确性不足以保证公平对齐，需考虑优化难度差异。CurriPO 通过课程学习利用多用户结构，有效解决优化公平性问题，为个性化 AI 对齐提供新范式。

Abstract: Learning a reward model from human feedback and optimizing a policy against it is one approach to aligning AI systems with individual users. From a fairness perspective, existing work improves such alignment by developing data-efficient and accurate reward models that capture minority preferences despite scarce data. We push this line of inquiry one step further and argue that data-efficient and accurate per-user reward models are not sufficient: users whose reward models are difficult to \textit{optimize} at the policy level can become a new underserved group. We start from the observation that one user's reward model can be easy to optimize from the initial policy while another's is not. We argue that, given a sufficiently diverse user population, a curriculum naturally emerges between easy- and hard-to-optimize reward models. Building on this insight, we propose CurriPO, which grows a tree-structured curriculum to accommodate diverse user-specific objectives, covering the population in a single traversal. Specifically, CurriPO automatically constructs a curriculum over diverse user reward models, allowing it to branch from the existing curriculum and reuse reward models previously incorporated into the curriculum. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to explicitly exploit multi-user structure to address optimization in AI alignment. Extensive experiments on personalized continuous control in a simulated environment show that CurriPO achieves $1.2$--$2.1\times$ the population satisfaction of the strongest baseline while substantially reducing training time. Additional analysis attributes much of this improvement to the users left underserved by conventional optimization.

</details>


### [136] [GraphK: Variable-Size Graph Generation with Efficient Edge Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18777)
*Resul Tugay,Eren Oluğ,Elif Ak,Sule Gunduz Oguducu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphK是一个新颖的编码器-采样器-解码器框架，用于图生成，通过结构灵活性和计算效率解决了可扩展性、灵活性和底层结构建模能力有限的问题。它支持图大小的上采样和下采样，学习排列不变的潜在表示，并利用KDTree-based top-k邻居搜索进行边预测，降低了计算成本。实验表明GraphK优于现有方法，能准确学习图结构并生成合成图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图生成模型虽然在深度学习方面取得了进展，但仍然受限于可扩展性、灵活性和建模底层结构的能力。自回归方法受词汇表大小（即图中的节点数）限制，难以灵活控制输出图的大小。因此需要一种能够跨图大小和结构泛化、且计算高效的图生成框架。

Method: GraphK采用编码器-采样器-解码器架构：编码器学习排列不变的节点潜在表示；采样器通过最大似然估计采样新的节点嵌入，支持图大小的上采样和下采样；解码器使用基于KDTree的top-k邻居搜索在潜在空间中进行边预测，基于流形平滑性假设捕捉图属性，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，GraphK优于现有方法，能够准确学习图结构，并生成无需显式定义的合成图。同时展示了跨图大小和结构的泛化能力，以及计算效率的提升。

Conclusion: GraphK通过结构灵活性和计算效率克服了现有图生成模型的局限性，提供了一种可扩展、灵活且高效的图生成解决方案，为图生成任务提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Graph generation models have advanced significantly with deep learning, yet they remain limited in scalability, flexibility, and ability to model underlying structures. We present GraphK, a novel encoder-sampler-decoder framework for graph generation that overcomes these challenges through structural flexibility and computational efficiency. Unlike autoregressive approaches constrained by vocabulary size (i.e. number of nodes in graph generation), GraphK allows for both upscaling (generating graphs with more nodes than the input) and downscaling, providing a flexible control over output graph size. By learning permutation-invariant latent representations and sampling new node embeddings via maximum likelihood estimation, GraphK generalizes across graph sizes and structures. For edge generation, we employ edge prediction with a KDTree-based top-k neighbor search in the latent space, reducing computational cost. Based on the manifold smoothness assumption, our method effectively captures graph properties. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that GraphK outperforms existing methods, accurately learns graph structures, and generates synthetic graphs without explicit definitions.

</details>


### [137] [A Real-Time Tsetlin Machine-based Non-intrusive Load Monitoring System on MCUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18780)
*Tianhang Tan,Han Wu,Tousif Rahman,Shengyu Duan,Alex Yakovlev,Rishad Shafik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Tsetlin Machine的NILM框架，将负荷分解问题重构为分类任务，实现了在资源受限单片机（ESP32）上的实时、隐私保护边缘部署，模型仅占用18KB闪存，推理延迟0.43ms，在REDD数据集上两设备分类精确率90%/召回率96%，四设备分类精确率77%/召回率80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NILM系统依赖计算密集型优化算法处理离线数据，难以部署在资源受限的单片机上进行本地实时推理，无法满足家庭敏感数据隐私保护和边缘计算的需求。

Method: 将NILM问题重构为分类任务，采用Tsetlin Machine（一种基于命题逻辑的可解释机器学习模型）构建轻量级分类器，针对ESP32等MCU平台进行模型压缩和优化，实现低内存占用和低延迟推理。

Result: 在REDD数据集上，两设备分类平均精确率90%、召回率96%；四设备分类精确率77%、召回率80%。模型仅占用18KB闪存，在ESP32上推理延迟0.43ms，验证了其在嵌入式NILM应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: 基于Tsetlin Machine的NILM框架成功实现了在极低资源单片机上的实时负荷分解，兼顾了分类性能、模型轻量化和隐私保护，为边缘侧能源监测提供了可行的部署方案。

Abstract: Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring (NILM) systems estimate individual appliance energy consumption from a single aggregate meter, without requiring separate sensors for each device. By installing a single meter that measures a building's total electricity consumption, NILM algorithms can determine the active status of each appliance. However, traditional NILM systems use computationally intensive optimization algorithms to process offline data, limiting their capability for on-device deployment, where sensitive household data must be processed locally. This paper proposes a Tsetlin Machine (TM)-based NILM framework, targeting real-time applications on resource-constrained microcontrollers (MCUs), enabling privacy-preserving edge deployment. The problem is reformulated as a classification task, and the proposed approach achieves an average precision of 90% and recall of 96% for two-appliance classification, and 77% precision and 80% recall for four appliances on the REDD dataset. The trained model occupies only 18 KB of flash memory and achieves an inference latency of 0.43 ms on an ESP32, demonstrating its suitability for embedded NILM applications on MCUs.

</details>


### [138] [Forgetting, plasticity, and co-observation: a third facet of continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18803)
*Timm Hess,Abhishek Jha,Gido M. van de Ven,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出持续学习中除了灾难性遗忘和可塑性丧失外，数据共观察是影响性能的关键因素，通过解耦数据访问约束，实证表明联合训练优于分离训练的性能差距源于共观察带来的泛化收益，且记忆重放的成功部分归因于其重新引入了共观察优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注灾难性遗忘和可塑性丧失，但无法完全解释朴素顺序训练与离线联合训练的性能差距。作者旨在揭示数据共观察作为独立因素对持续学习性能的影响，填补理论空白。

Method: 通过解耦单独数据访问约束与稳定性/可塑性，在监督和自监督范式下，设计通用数据增量“分块”场景，对比联合训练与分离训练，同时缓解遗忘并控制可塑性，系统研究共观察带来的表征收益。

Result: 实证发现联合训练与分离训练存在一致性能差距，共观察带来的泛化收益超越单纯知识保留，且不依赖特定分布偏移。蒸馏方法仅作为知识保留机制，而记忆重放的成功超越遗忘缓解，主动重引入共观察优势。

Conclusion: 数据共观察是持续学习中被忽视的关键因素，其带来的泛化收益独立于遗忘缓解和可塑性维持。未来持续学习方法设计应显式考虑如何重现或近似共观察带来的表征优势。

Abstract: Efficient continual learning remains a fundamental challenge for deep neural networks. While catastrophic forgetting and loss of plasticity are widely considered the primary obstacles to overcome, we show that these two issues cannot fully explain the performance gap between naive sequential training and offline joint training. In this paper, we highlight data co-observation as a distinct factor influencing continual learning performance. By decoupling the constraints of separate data access from stability and plasticity, we systematically investigate the representational benefits gained by observing training data together. Empirically, we demonstrate a consistent performance difference between joint and separate training across both supervised and self-supervised paradigms in generic data-incremental "chunking" scenarios, whilst mitigating forgetting and controlling for plasticity. Our findings indicate that simultaneous observation of training data (co-observation) yields benefits to the learner's generalization that extend well beyond mere knowledge retention, and that this effect does not require a specific continual distribution shift. Furthermore, we contextualize prominent continual learning mechanisms through this lens: while distillation-based approaches act only as effective knowledge retention mechanisms, our results suggest that the empirical success of memory replay goes beyond the mitigation of forgetting, actively reintroducing the benefits of data co-observation into the learning process.

</details>


### [139] [Tensor Field Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18808)
*Alexander Strunk,Roland Assam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出张量场模型，一种在生成流形上通过算子将组件族映射为时变切向量场的生成建模框架，采用流匹配训练，实验显示性能提升且可复用条件表示加速采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在结构化条件建模、组件解耦与复用、以及几何一致性方面存在局限。作者旨在构建一个数学化、模块化的生成框架，将解析与动力学约束编码进可容许组件族的选择中，而非硬编码在根定义里，从而实现更灵活、可解释且高效的条件生成。

Method: 定义张量场模型：学习算子将可容许组件截面族的乘积映射为生成状态流形上规定的时变切截面族。提供三种结构化细化：构造型、组件可分离型、张量丛 TFM。条件实现中，结构化条件逐分量映射为可复用集合 H_c，组件表示保持分离，仅由场算子组合生成向量场。所有模型均采用流匹配训练。

Result: 实验表明 TFM 能提升生成性能；得益于可复用条件表示，摊销采样显著加速生成过程。

Conclusion: TFM 为生成建模提供了几何严谨、模块化且可扩展的统一框架，通过将约束内化于组件族选择与算子设计，兼顾了表达力、条件复用效率与理论可分析性，为后续几何深度生成研究奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces Tensor Field Models (TFMs), realization-level Mathematical Structures in which a learned Operator maps a product of admissible component-section families to a prescribed family of time-dependent tangent sections on a Generative State Manifold. Analytic and dynamical restrictions are encoded through the choice of admissible families rather than imposed by the root definition. Constructed, component-separable, and Tensor Bundle TFMs provide structured refinements of this common object. In the conditional realizations considered here, a structured condition $c=(c_1,\ldots,c_n)$ is mapped componentwise to a reusable collection $\mathbf H_c=(H_{c_1}^{(1)},\ldots,H_{c_n}^{(n)})$. In the architectures evaluated here, the component representations remain distinct and are combined only by the Field Operator to produce the generated Vector Field. All learned models are trained using Flow Matching. Experiments show that TFMs can improve performance and that amortized sampling enabled by reusable condition representations can accelerate generation.

</details>


### [140] [Many Optimizers But Only One Training Path: Repeated Resampling for Adaptive Optimizer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18810)
*Ronald Richman,Mario V. Wüthrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Repeated Optimizer Resampling (ROR)方法，在单次训练运行中动态搜索优化器，通过短周期侦察比较候选优化器，大幅降低寻找最佳优化器的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习训练前固定优化器，将优化器选择视为超参数需多次完整训练并丢弃失败者，计算开销大。ROR旨在一次演化运行中搜索优化器，允许优化器随训练进程自适应变化。

Method: ROR每b个周期让各候选优化器从当前权重出发侦察s个周期，选最佳侦察者继续剩余b-s周期，若改善验证目标则成为新现任优化器。实验在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST及两个机动车保险理赔计数模型上对比两种ROR变体与九个固定优化器，使用相同十个随机种子。

Result: One-epoch ROR仅需穷举识别最佳固定优化器所需总训练量的24%~35%，在四个任务上性能均接近最佳固定优化器。

Conclusion: 短侦察机制是一种实用的优化器搜索策略，无需完成每个候选优化器的完整训练即可有效选出高性能优化器。
